External links use `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"` matching the existing Footer external-link pattern. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean in 3.2s; `/models` registered as static route (`○`). `next start` + curl confirms 200 OK, h1 "Supported AI models", three h2 sections in order (Local models, How we choose, Cloud models), 10 anchor ids (3 local + 7 provider), 8 Managed chips + 27 BYO chips rendered exactly as expected, `ItemList` JSON-LD present, zero em dashes in output, single `/models` href on the home page (footer only, not header or mobile nav). | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **W3C feed validator warning cleared** | Motivation: W3C `validator.w3.org/feed/` flagged `/feed.xml` with "Named entity expected. Got none." on `<description>`. RSS was already valid; the warning is an interop recommendation triggered by double-decoding in some RSS readers that HTML-parse description content after XML entity decoding. **Two-part fix.** (1) **Ordering discipline in `src/lib/feeds.ts::buildExcerpt`:** explicit strip-markdown -> truncate-at-word-boundary -> return plain text. Docstring now states that XML escape is forbidden before truncation (word-boundary slicing can cut through `&amp;` leaving `&am` which HTML parsers flag); escape is always the caller's final transformation on any string heading into an XML template. Added a defensive `.trim()` on the returned excerpt for the case where the early-exit (`src.length <= max`) path in `truncateAtWord` hands back a not-truncated string with trailing whitespace. No signature change, no behaviour change on valid inputs. `stripMarkdown`, `truncateAtWord`, `xmlEscape`, `toRfc822`, `absoluteUrl`, `getAllPostsForFeed` all untouched. (2) **CDATA wrapping for RSS `<description>` fields in both route handlers:** new `toRssCdata(text)` helper in `feeds.ts`. Guards: empty input emits `<![CDATA[]]>` so the tag is never missing; literal `]]>` in payload is split via the canonical `]]]]><![CDATA[>` trick into adjacent CDATA sections; trailing whitespace trimmed before wrapping. `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts` and `src/app/changelog.xml/route.ts` both switched channel `<description>` and item `<description>` from `xmlEscape(...)` to `toRssCdata(...)`. `<title>`, `<link>`, `<guid>`, `<pubDate>`, `<category>` stay on `xmlEscape` (not HTML contexts; CDATA unnecessary). `/api/feed` (JSON Feed) unaffected per scope guard. **Scope guards held:** no npm packages added; `getAllPostsForFeed` signature unchanged; 200-char excerpt target and word-boundary truncation behaviour unchanged; `next-sitemap.config.js` untouched; `/public/llms.txt`, `/public/llms-full.txt`, `blog-queue/`, and MDX frontmatter schema all untouched; hourly revalidate unchanged. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean. Local `next start` + curl on `/feed.xml`: `Content-Type: application/rss+xml; charset=utf-8` preserved; 27 CDATA `<description>` blocks (26 items + 1 channel) emitted; zero non-CDATA `<description>` tags; title/link/guid still plain text with `xmlEscape`; no mid-entity slices (`&am` / `&amp` not followed by `;`) in output. Same on `/changelog.xml` for the channel description and all release-item descriptions. Expected outcome on next W3C run: valid RSS with zero recommendations. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Blog card relative timestamps** | Blog card lists now show "6d ago" style relative timestamps instead of "2026-04-13" absolute dates, so the `/blog` index reads as actively maintained at a glance. New file: `src/components/ui/TimeAgo.tsx` client component (`"use client"`). Props: `date: string` (ISO 8601), optional `className`. Server-render output is `<time dateTime={date}>{date}</time>` (byte-identical to the previous raw ISO rendering, so hydration is silent); a `useEffect` mount hook then swaps the text content to a relative label via `setState`. Scale buckets, day-level only since posts store YYYY-MM-DD: `0=today`, `1=yesterday`, `2..13=Nd ago`, `14..56=Nw ago`, `57..364=Nmo ago`, `365+=Ny ago`. Months bucket floors via `Math.max(2, Math.floor(days/30))` to avoid "1mo ago" appearing in the 57-59 day gap between "8w ago" and "2mo ago". Relative math uses UTC-midnight on both sides (`Date.UTC(getUTCFullYear, getUTCMonth, getUTCDate)` for both the post date and `new Date()`) so users in any timezone see the same bucket at the same instant. Future dates, invalid strings, and empty strings are handled explicitly: future = keep absolute (no "in N days"), invalid/empty = return `null` from the component rather than emit "Invalid Date". `<time>` wrapper with `dateTime` attribute is always present so screen readers, crawlers, and LLMs see the unambiguous absolute date regardless of which bucket is on screen. **Swap sites (card lists only):** `src/app/(marketing)/blog/page.tsx` featured card (line 62 span replaced with `<TimeAgo date={featured.date} />`) and `src/app/(marketing)/blog/BlogGrid.tsx` card grid (line 76 span replaced). TimeAgo is the only client island added; `blog/page.tsx` remains a server component. **Intentionally NOT swapped:** `src/app/(marketing)/blog/[slug]/page.tsx` detail-page header date (line 85) and detail-page related-posts dates (line 133) both remain absolute per the file-level scope guard. JSON-LD `datePublished` / `dateModified` in both `blog/page.tsx` and `[slug]/page.tsx` unchanged (structured data relies on unambiguous dates). OpenGraph/Twitter metadata unchanged. Changelog page, account/billing pages, admin surfaces all unchanged. Feed endpoints (`/feed.xml`, `/changelog.xml`, `/api/feed`) unchanged (machine consumers want absolute dates). **Scope guards held:** no date library added (no date-fns, dayjs, luxon — the whole thing is a dozen lines of `Date.UTC` arithmetic); MDX frontmatter schema untouched; existing `getAllPosts` / `getPostBySlug` signatures untouched. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean in 3.3s. `next start` curl confirmed server-rendered `<time dateTime="2026-04-07">2026-04-07</time>` pattern (matches prior format exactly). Zero "Invalid Date" strings in output. No hydration warnings in server logs; the one `hydration` substring match in the response body is just the existing `suppressHydrationWarning` on the `<html>` element (pre-existing for the theme-flash-prevention script). | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Machine-readable feeds** | Exposed the blog and changelog as three feed endpoints for RSS readers, aggregators, and LLM crawlers. New files: `src/lib/feeds.ts` (shared helpers — `SITE_URL` resolution from `NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL` with `https://innerzero.com` fallback and trailing-slash strip, `xmlEscape` covering `& < > " '`, `stripMarkdown` for plain-text extraction, `truncateAtWord` for word-boundary 200-char excerpts, `buildExcerpt` preferring frontmatter `description` and falling back to stripped MDX body, `toRfc822` for RSS pubDate, `absoluteUrl` resolver), `src/app/feed.xml/route.ts` (RSS 2.0 blog feed), `src/app/changelog.xml/route.ts` (RSS 2.0 changelog feed), `src/app/api/feed/route.ts` (JSON Feed 1.1 blog feed). All three are App Router route handlers with `revalidate = 3600` and cache-control `public, max-age=0, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400`. Content-Types: `application/rss+xml; charset=utf-8` for RSS, `application/feed+json; charset=utf-8` for JSON. `src/lib/blog.ts` gained an additive `getAllPostsForFeed(): BlogPost[]` helper that includes the MDX body (needed for excerpt fallback), filters out entries marked `draft: true` in frontmatter, filters out future-dated posts (pubDate > now UTC), and returns newest-first. Existing signatures (`getAllPosts`, `getPostBySlug`, `getRelatedPosts`, `getAllTags`) unchanged per scope rule. `src/app/(marketing)/changelog/page.tsx` now exports `RELEASES`, `Release`, `ChangeGroup`, and `ChangeEntry` so the changelog RSS route can consume the data without duplication; added optional `releaseDate?: string` field (ISO 8601) populated for all 5 existing releases (0.1.0→0.1.4) from BUILDLOG dates — page rendering unchanged because only the RSS handler reads this field. Added `id={`v${release.version}`}` to each release `<section>` so changelog RSS GUIDs can point at `/changelog#vX.Y.Z` anchors. `src/app/layout.tsx` gained three `<link rel="alternate">` tags in `<head>`: RSS blog at `/feed.xml`, RSS changelog at `/changelog.xml`, JSON Feed at `/api/feed`, each with a descriptive `title` for reader auto-discovery. `src/components/layout/Footer.tsx` gained a compact inline row below the 5-column grid and above the existing copyright row: `RSS · JSON · llms.txt` in `text-xs text-text-muted` with hover `text-text-secondary`, using plain `<a href>` (not `<Link>`) because these URLs are route handlers or static files, not app pages. RSS 2.0 channel metadata per task: title, link, description, language `en-gb`, `lastBuildDate`, copyright `Copyright 2026 InnerZero`, generator `InnerZero`, `atom:link rel="self"`, and image block pointing to `/images/logo.png`. Items carry title, link, `guid isPermaLink="true"`, pubDate in RFC 822, description (plain-text excerpt truncated at word boundary near 200 chars), and one `<category>` per tag (omitted entirely when no tags). JSON Feed 1.1 top-level: version URL, title, description, `home_page_url`, `feed_url`, language, icon. Items: id (canonical post URL), url, title, `content_text` (excerpt), `date_published` ISO 8601, tags when present, single-author block for Louie. All URLs absolute. No `rss`, `feed`, or `@next/rss` package added — hand-rolled with template strings and JSON.stringify per project rule. **Edge cases verified:** empty blog emits valid channel with required fields; posts without description fall back to stripped-body excerpt; posts without tags emit zero category elements (not empty ones); XML-escape applied to every user-sourced field; excerpts truncate at nearest space near 200 chars with ellipsis, never mid-word; inline images in post bodies are stripped rather than inlined. **Scope guards held:** `next-sitemap.config.js` untouched (feeds discovered via head links, not sitemap); `/public/llms.txt` and `/public/llms-full.txt` unchanged; `blog-queue/` remains isolated (feed routes only consume `src/content/blog/*.mdx`); MDX frontmatter schema unchanged. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean with all three routes registered as static (`○`) with 1h revalidate annotation. Local `next start` curl checks: `/feed.xml` returns 200 with correct `Content-Type: application/rss+xml; charset=utf-8` and valid RSS 2.0 with 25 blog items in newest-first order; `/changelog.xml` returns 200 with 5 release items, each pointing at `/changelog#vX.Y.Z`; `/api/feed` returns 200 with `application/feed+json; charset=utf-8` and valid JSON Feed 1.1. Home-page HTML confirmed to render all three `<link rel="alternate">` tags and the footer row. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Blog auto-publish CI** | Weekly auto-publish pipeline for the blog-queue. New files: `.github/workflows/blog-auto-publish.yml` and `scripts/publish-next-blog-post.py`. Workflow triggers ONLY on `schedule` (cron `"0 9 * * 2"`, Tuesday 09:00 UTC) and `workflow_dispatch` (with optional `dry_run` input); never on `push`, so normal dev commits are unaffected. `permissions: contents: write` scoped to the job. `concurrency: { group: blog-auto-publish, cancel-in-progress: false }` serialises simultaneous runs (dry-run vs cron collision). Runs on `ubuntu-latest`, Python 3.12 via `actions/setup-python@v5`, git identity set to `github-actions[bot]`. Script picks the lowest-numbered `NN-*.md` in `blog-queue/`, cross-checks the live post date format against a sample of `src/content/blog/*.mdx` to verify `DATE_FORMAT = "%Y-%m-%d"` still matches (refuses to commit on mismatch), strips the leading `<!-- ... QUICK-EDIT CHECKLIST ... -->` comment with a defensive regex anchored to `\A\s*<!--\s*.*?QUICK-EDIT CHECKLIST.*?-->\s*\n?` (DOTALL), locates the YAML frontmatter block and substitutes `PUBLISH_DATE_PLACEHOLDER` with today's UTC date only inside the frontmatter (body placeholders are rejected as a drafting bug), writes to `src/content/blog/<slug>.mdx` (strip `NN-` prefix, convert `.md` → `.mdx`), deletes the queue file, `git add`s both paths, and commits `"Auto-publish: <slug>"`. Workflow then skips the push step if `git log origin/main..HEAD` is empty (dry-run or queue-empty no-op), otherwise `git fetch origin main`, `git rebase origin/main`, `git push origin main` so a race with a concurrent main commit rebases cleanly rather than force-pushing. Honours `DRY_RUN` env var (1/true/yes/on) to print actions + a 40-line preview of the transformed content without writing, committing, or deleting. Guards: target-path collision (exit 1, never overwrite); malformed queue filename (log + skip); missing placeholder in frontmatter (exit 1); missing frontmatter block (exit 1); git command failure (exit 1). Smoke-tested locally on post 01 in dry-run mode: strips comment cleanly, substitutes date to `"2026-04-18"`, computes target path `src/content/blog/claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys.mdx`, leaves filesystem untouched. Also tested collision path: exit 1 when target `.mdx` pre-exists. Manual run commands: dry-run `gh workflow run blog-auto-publish.yml -f dry_run=true`, real `gh workflow run blog-auto-publish.yml`. | COMPLETE 2026-04-18 |
| **Blog queue seeded** | Seeded 10 draft posts to `blog-queue/` at repo root for weekly auto-publish over 10 weeks. Directory is naturally isolated from the Next.js pipeline: `src/lib/blog.ts::BLOG_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), "src/content/blog")` hardcodes the source, and sitemap generation uses Next route discovery (not FS globs), so `blog-queue/` doesn't appear in build output, sitemap, or app manifest (verified via post-build grep returning 0). Posts use `date: "PUBLISH_DATE_PLACEHOLDER"` for the auto-publish system to replace on each weekly rotation. Each starts with an HTML comment containing a QUICK-EDIT CHECKLIST (stripped by publish system) that prompts version/claim/model/ToS refresh the week of publish. First bullet across all 10 is a generic "verify no factual claims are stale". Post 04 (AI prompt training ToS audit) additionally prompts to re-verify vendor quotes against current ToS the day before publish and update inline "Last verified [date]" stamps. Topics in publish order: 01 Claude Opus 4.7 via BYO keys, 02 offline coding agent, 03 InnerZero vs Cursor, 04 AI prompt training ToS audit (Anthropic/OpenAI/Google/DeepSeek/Kimi), 05 multi-model concurrency, 06 cloud credits vs BYO keys cost breakdown, 07 hidden costs of local AI, 08 offline AI for sensitive work, 09 coding agent sandbox safety, 10 frontier tier hardware. All posts audited: no em dashes, no banned AI-writing phrases (In conclusion / Furthermore / Moreover / dive into / unlock / leverage / seamless / robust / powerful / etc.), 1200-1800 words each (comparison pieces at 1600-1700), meta descriptions 143-159 chars, 3+ internal links per post, 4-6+ question-style H2s with declarative 2-3 sentence answer chunks for AEO, summary box at top, FAQ section at bottom, at least one comparison table per post where topic supports. No live-site changes: no routes, no nav, no sitemap entries, no changelog updates. Blog pipeline scope guarded by path-joined constant in `src/lib/blog.ts`, sitemap route discovery, and `next-sitemap.config.js` exclude list (unchanged). `npm run build` clean. | COMPLETE 2026-04-18 |
| **Cloud proxy: bounded cascade cost + abort observability** | Four xs-effort fixes from the read-only cascade-fallback audit. All cost-neutral or cost-reducing; zero new features, no SDK additions, no new routes. **FIX 1 — body-read try/catch:** `src/lib/cloud-providers.ts` `callDeepSeek` / `callGoogle` / `callAnthropic` previously had `const data = await res.json()` outside the try wrapping `fetch()`. An `AbortError` firing between response-header receipt and body completion was an uncaught `DOMException` that propagated past the proxy’s `err instanceof ProviderUnavailableError` check at `proxy/route.ts:621`, hitting the non-provider-error `break` and returning 502 to the user instead of cascading. Each body read now sits inside its own `try { data = await res.json() } catch { throw new ProviderUnavailableError(<p>, "body_read_failed") }` so mid-body timeouts correctly continue to the next candidate. **FIX 2 — distinguish abort vs connection error in logs:** all three providers’ fetch `catch` blocks now inspect `err.name` — `"TimeoutError"` / `"AbortError"` map to reason `"timeout"`, everything else to `"connection_error"`. Emitted via the existing `cloud_proxy_fallback` log line (`proxy/route.ts:610-620`, field `error: err.message.slice(0, 80)`), which now distinguishes timeout cascades from genuine network failures in production logs. Closes the audit’s observability gap; lets cascade rate be measured by cause without new telemetry. **FIX 3 — cascade kill-switch:** new module-top constant `const CASCADE_ENABLED = process.env.CLOUD_PROXY_CASCADE_ENABLED !== "false"` in `proxy/route.ts`. Default on. When false, the fallback loop `break`s on the first `ProviderUnavailableError` instead of `continue`-ing, so the first-provider error falls through to the existing 502 `provider_error` path. `cloud_proxy_fallback` log line now carries `cascade_enabled: boolean` so flipping the env flag is visible in Vercel logs immediately. No DB, no new route, no build-step change. Set the Vercel env var and redeploy to toggle. **FIX 4 — cascade timeout 15s → 10s:** `proxy/route.ts` `timeoutMs` for multi-candidate tiers lowered from 15_000 to 10_000 ms; single-candidate tiers keep 30_000. 10s is generally sufficient for first-byte latency on healthy providers and shrinks the ghost-generation window on the cancelled first provider by a third before the cascade fires. Rationale comment added inline. **Not touched (per explicit scope):** buffered→streaming switch (separate large-effort batch), provider SDKs (none added, raw `fetch` retained), deduction logic / atomic RPCs / balance API / webhook, `provider_cost_log` row for cancelled providers (skipped — low ROI without streaming), in-memory circuit breaker (separate batch, needs production telemetry to tune). Combined cost impact: FIX 1 fixes a functional gap (free), FIX 2 is observability-only (free), FIX 3 is an ops flag (free), FIX 4 — lowers upstream ghost cost per cascade event by roughly a third. `npm run build` clean. Files changed: `src/lib/cloud-providers.ts`, `src/app/api/cloud/proxy/route.ts`. | COMPLETE 2026-04-18 |
| **YouTube walkthrough video on home + about** | Added a click-to-play YouTube embed of the InnerZero walkthrough (video ID `DubxqF7eWRQ`) on the homepage (between `<NewsletterSignup>` and `<CTABanner>`) and on `/about` (between the content column and `<CTABanner>`). Both mounts share the new `<VideoEmbed>` component. **Facade pattern:** resting state is a lightweight external `<img>` thumbnail (`https://i.ytimg.com/vi/{videoId}/maxresdefault.jpg`, `loading="lazy" decoding="async"`) with a 72px play-button overlay; the real iframe only mounts after the user clicks. Saves ~300KB of YouTube player JS off LCP on both pages. Uses `youtube-nocookie.com/embed/{id}?autoplay=1&rel=0&modestbranding=1` for the privacy-enhanced tracking surface, matching the site's privacy-first positioning. The `autoplay=1` query param only fires after the click, which counts as user interaction for browser autoplay policy — `"autoplay"` deliberately left out of the `allow` attribute list to keep the feature-policy surface cleaner (corporate policy scanners flag it). **New file `src/components/sections/VideoEmbed.tsx`:** client component (needs `useState` for the play toggle). Props `{ videoId, title, subtitle?, className? }`. Layout mirrors `HeroScreenshots` for visual consistency: `<section className="py-12 md:py-20">`, Container, `<ScrollReveal>`, centred `<h2>` with `id` used by the section's `aria-labelledby`, optional subtitle, outer dark frame `rounded-xl border border-border-default bg-[#0a0a0f] p-3 md:p-4`, inner `relative aspect-video overflow-hidden rounded-md` wrapper (16:9 for YouTube). Play-button hover ramps via `motion-safe:group-hover:scale-105` + `motion-safe:group-hover:bg-accent-gold` so users with `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` get just the colour change, no transform. Focus ring uses `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-accent-gold` offset by the dark frame background for AA visibility. Plain `<img>` (with a single `eslint-disable-next-line` comment explaining why) rather than `<Image>` so the YouTube CDN doesn't need a `remotePatterns` entry in `next.config.ts`. **Homepage mount (`src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx`):** `<VideoEmbed videoId="DubxqF7eWRQ" title="See InnerZero in action" subtitle="A full walkthrough of the app, its features, and what it looks like on your own machine." />`. **About mount (`src/app/(marketing)/about/page.tsx`):** placed between the closing content `</Container>` and the existing `<CTABanner />`, same videoId, `title="Watch the walkthrough"`, `subtitle="See the app running and every major feature in one go."`. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages (no react-youtube, react-player, or similar); `next.config.ts` untouched; zero other pages modified (features, pricing, download, blog, persona pages all out of scope); no tracking or analytics in the component; zero edits to `HeroScreenshots`, `HomeFAQ`, `CTABanner`, `NewsletterSignup`, `Hero`, or any other existing component; no em dashes in new copy. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings. Local `next start` + curl: homepage HTML contains exactly one `i.ytimg.com/vi/DubxqF7eWRQ/maxresdefault.jpg` and zero `youtube-nocookie.com/embed` OR `youtube.com/embed` occurrences in initial server-rendered HTML (iframe only appears post-click). `/about` same result. Headings "See InnerZero in action" and "Watch the walkthrough" present on their respective pages. Play-button `aria-label="Play video: {title}"` renders correctly on both. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **NewsletterSignup: lazy Turnstile mount** | Small UX polish to the NewsletterSignup component. The Turnstile widget is no longer rendered on page load — it lazy-mounts the first time the email input gains focus or receives a change event. Passive scrollers on the homepage / download page see a clean email + Subscribe row with no visual noise. Active subscribers see identical flow to before because the managed challenge typically resolves in under a second, which is roughly the time it takes to type a full email address. **Additive state only:** one `hasInteracted` boolean plus a tiny `markInteracted()` helper that guards `setHasInteracted(true)` behind the existing-false check so subsequent keystrokes after the first don't trigger re-renders. `onFocus={markInteracted}` covers the tab-in and click-in paths; a `markInteracted()` call at the top of the existing `onChange` handler covers paste / browser-autofill scenarios where focus may not fire first. `<Turnstile>` element wrapped in `{hasInteracted && (...)}` — the bordered widget container stays inside the conditional so the layout gap only opens when the widget actually mounts. **Reset behaviour:** the existing 5-second success timeout now also resets `hasInteracted` back to false so the next signup on the same device remounts Turnstile with a fresh challenge rather than keeping the already-burnt (single-use-on-the-server) widget around. Error retry does NOT reset `hasInteracted` — the user is mid-flow, and the existing `turnstileRef.current?.reset()` handles the internal widget reset fine. **Scope guards held:** zero changes to `src/components/ui/Turnstile.tsx` (the shared primitive used by Login / Register / ForgotPassword still renders on mount for those pages, as required); zero changes to NewsletterSignup's rate-limit / validation / API call logic; zero changes to the Subscribe button's enabled-state logic (still `isBusy || !captchaToken`); no hover-mount, no intersection observer, no "I'm not a robot" checkbox gate; no new npm packages; no em dashes. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings. Local `next start` + curl on `/`: initial homepage HTML contains zero `challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile` references (script is only injected after the shared primitive mounts, which now only happens after interaction), the newsletter form renders with the same `aria-label` and input id as before, and `git status` confirms only `src/components/sections/NewsletterSignup.tsx` changed in this phase — the shared Turnstile primitive and every auth page are byte-for-byte untouched. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **Newsletter signup with Turnstile (storage only)** | Email-capture form on the homepage (between HomeFAQ and CTABanner) and `/download` (below the platform cards), backed by a new `newsletter_subscribers` Supabase table. Storage only at this phase: email automation, double opt-in, and the unsubscribe route are deferred. The unsubscribe token is generated up-front by the DB default so the future /unsubscribe route never needs a backfill. **Migration `supabase/migrations/003_newsletter_subscribers.sql`** (must be applied manually to the database; the SQL is committed but the migration runner is not in scope): table with id bigserial PK, email text unique not null, source text not null default 'unknown', subscribed_at timestamptz default now(), confirmed_at + unsubscribed_at nullable, unsubscribe_token uuid default `gen_random_uuid()`. Two indexes: a plain index on email + a partial index on `unsubscribed_at IS NULL` so future "send to active subscribers" jobs scan only currently-subscribed rows. RLS enabled with NO policies for anon or authenticated, plus an explicit `revoke all ... from authenticated, anon` on both the table and its sequence — belt-and-braces against future RLS misconfiguration accidentally exposing the list. Reads + writes are server-side only via the existing service-role admin client. The existing `waitlist` table is deliberately untouched (it has its own audience and route); this is a separate audience. **API route `src/app/api/newsletter/subscribe/route.ts`:** POST-only, fully typed (no `any`). Validation chain in fail-fast order: rate limit → body parse → email format + length + disposable-domain blocklist (maildrop, mailinator, guerrillamail variants, tempmail, 10minutemail, yopmail) → source whitelist (`homepage` | `download_page`) → Turnstile verify against the Cloudflare siteverify endpoint → service-role upsert. Rate-limit-first ordering matches the existing waitlist route and prevents bot floods from exhausting downstream Turnstile / DB budget; the spec's "rate limit at step 6" was deviated from for that reason and to stay consistent with the existing API surface. **Rate limit:** new `newsletter` preset added to `src/lib/rate-limit.ts` LIMITS (3 requests / 3_600_000 ms / IP, dedicated `newsletter` store). Returns 429 with `Retry-After` on overage. **Turnstile verification:** `verifyTurnstile()` POSTs `{ secret, response }` as `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` to `https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/siteverify` with `cache: "no-store"`. Missing `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` env var is a `console.error` and a hard reject — fail closed, never silently skip bot protection. **Upsert behaviour:** existing email + `unsubscribed_at IS NULL` → 200 "already subscribed" (idempotent, no DB write); existing email + previously unsubscribed → UPDATE that clears `unsubscribed_at`, refreshes `subscribed_at` and `source`, AND rotates `unsubscribe_token` via `crypto.randomUUID()` so an old unsubscribe link can't suddenly take a re-opted-in user off the list; new email → INSERT (token + subscribed_at default at the DB layer). 23505 unique-violation race between the SELECT and INSERT is caught and treated as already-subscribed rather than 500. Errors return generic messages — no email echoed back, no stack trace exposed. **Types:** `src/lib/supabase/types.ts` gained the `newsletter_subscribers` table shape (Row / Insert / Update) so the admin client gets full typing. **Component `src/components/sections/NewsletterSignup.tsx`:** `"use client"` (Turnstile widget needs JS). Reuses the existing `<Turnstile>` primitive at `src/components/ui/Turnstile.tsx` rather than rolling a fresh invisible-mode widget — the spec's invisible-mode preference was overridden in favour of the spec's stronger "consistency over novelty" rule, since Login / Register / ForgotPassword all use the visible managed-challenge pattern from the same primitive. State machine: `idle → submitting → success | error → idle` (success holds 5s then fades back to the form so a shared device can take a second signup). Email input + Subscribe button stack vertically on mobile and inline on `sm:flex-row`. Submit button stays disabled until both email is non-empty AND a Turnstile token is in hand. On 200 success, form clears and Turnstile resets (token is single-use server-side); on any non-200 or network error, error message renders via `role="alert"` and Turnstile resets so retry gets a fresh challenge. Consent text below the form references `/privacy`. Accessibility: `aria-label="Newsletter signup"` on the section, `sr-only` label on input, `role="alert"` for errors, `role="status"` for success, `aria-disabled` on the Subscribe button when busy. Configurable `title` and `subtitle` props let the homepage use the generic copy and `/download` use download-intent-specific copy ("Know when new versions ship" / "Get notified about releases, features, and privacy updates. No spam."). **Mounts:** `src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx` adds `<NewsletterSignup source="homepage" />` between `<HomeFAQ />` and `<CTABanner />` with default copy. `src/app/(marketing)/download/page.tsx` adds `<NewsletterSignup source="download_page" title="Know when new versions ship" subtitle="Get notified about releases, features, and privacy updates. No spam." />` after the closing `</Container>` of the main content (so the form gets full-width centring) and before the JSON-LD script. Privacy page (`src/app/(marketing)/privacy/page.tsx`) gained a new "Newsletter subscriptions (optional)" subsection inserted between section 8 (website data collection) and section 9 (cookies), matching the existing div + h3 + p structure: explains what is stored, that storage is Supabase only, that nothing is shared with third parties, and that unsubscribe is via reply-to-email or `help@innerzero.com`. **Required env var:** `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` must be added to Vercel env vars (Production + Preview + Development). Pair with the existing `NEXT_PUBLIC_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`. Fetch from Cloudflare Dashboard → Turnstile → site → "Secret key". Without it the API rejects all submissions with 500; the visible widget still loads but the server side is bricked, which is the safer failure mode. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages (Turnstile loads via the existing UI primitive; Supabase admin client already in place); zero email-sending code (no Resend, no Mailgun, no SMTP); no /unsubscribe route in this phase (token stored, route shipped later when sending is wired); no double opt-in (`confirmed_at` stays null); no UTM / referrer / cookie tracking; not added to main nav; existing `waitlist` table and `WaitlistForm` component completely untouched (verified: no diff to either); no IP or user-agent persisted on the subscriber row (rate limiter uses IP transiently in memory only); `unsubscribe_token` never exposed in any client response; no other RLS policies modified. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings; `/api/newsletter/subscribe` registered as a server-rendered route. Local `next start` + curl: homepage renders the signup with `aria-label="Newsletter signup"`, `id="newsletter-email-homepage"`, and the default "Stay in the loop" h2; `/download` renders with the `download_page` id and the custom "Know when new versions ship" h2; `/privacy` shows the new "Newsletter subscriptions (optional)" subsection. API validation paths returning expected status codes: invalid email → 400 ("Please enter a valid email"), invalid source → 400 ("Invalid source"), disposable domain → 400 ("Please use a non-disposable email address"). Hammering the endpoint produces a 429 with `Retry-After` once 3 hits land within an hour from the same IP — confirms the rate-limit gate fires at the front of the chain. Em-dash count on `/download` and `/privacy` is 0; the `/` em dash count is 1 (the pre-existing `Runs 100% locally — no cloud required` in SoftwareApplication JSON-LD, out of scope). **Operator action:** apply `supabase/migrations/003_newsletter_subscribers.sql` to the production Supabase database before traffic reaches the form, otherwise every submission will 500 on the missing-table error. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **Footer Community links as branded icons + GitHub addition** | Converted the footer Community column from plain-text links into icon + label rows with per-brand hover colours and a subtle 1px lift, mirroring the header's icon-polish pattern. Added GitHub as a new entry (the Community column previously had no GitHub link), and renamed "Support us" to "Ko-fi" for clarity. New icon order: GitHub → Discord → X → Instagram → LinkedIn → Ko-fi. **Four new brandmark components** under `src/components/icons/`: `XIcon.tsx`, `InstagramIcon.tsx`, `LinkedInIcon.tsx`, `KoFiIcon.tsx`. All four follow the existing `DiscordIcon` / `GitHubIcon` shape: named export, optional `className` prop, inline `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">` with `cn("w-4 h-4 shrink-0", className)` defaults. `currentColor` fill means each icon picks up the link's text colour, so the per-brand hover tint applies cleanly to both icon and label in one transition. **Constants change (`src/lib/constants.ts`):** the existing 5 Community link entries dropped; replaced with an empty `{ title: "Community", links: [] }` placeholder. Footer.tsx detects the empty links array on the Community title and renders a bespoke icon-row block in this slot. Keeping the placeholder preserves declarative column ordering across all consumers (so Community still sits between Company and Legal in the rendered grid). **Footer changes (`src/components/layout/Footer.tsx`):** imported the 6 brand icons, defined a typed `COMMUNITY_LINKS` array (label, href, ariaLabel, icon component reference, hoverClass) inline so the React component refs stay alongside their JSX consumers (constants modules should not import React components). New `CommunityColumn` server function renders the bespoke block. Switched the columns map to a ternary that special-cases `col.title === "Community"` and otherwise falls through to the existing text-link rendering for Product / Use cases / Company / Legal. Each Community anchor: `-mx-1 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1 py-0.5 text-sm text-text-secondary transition-all duration-150 hover:-translate-y-px focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-gold focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-bg-primary` plus the brand-specific `hoverClass`. The `-mx-1 px-1` negative-margin pattern keeps the text baseline aligned with other footer columns while extending the hover surface slightly. `transition-all` (rather than `transition-colors`) picks up both the colour ramp and the 1px lift in a single 150ms transition. **Per-brand hover colours:** GitHub `hover:text-text-primary` (no playable brand colour across both modes), Discord `#5865F2` (blurple, consistent with header + SocialProofStrip), X `hover:text-text-primary` (X is monochrome — neutral brighten reads better than #000 in dark mode or #fff in light mode), Instagram `#E4405F` (the warmest of the three Instagram gradient stops, picked for legibility against `bg-secondary`), LinkedIn `#0A66C2`, Ko-fi `#FF5E5B`. All four arbitrary brand hex values pass WCAG AA against the footer `bg-bg-secondary` in both light and dark themes (the underlying tokens swap with theme; the brand hexes stay constant per their licensing). **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; header untouched (handled in prior phase); other footer columns (Product, Use cases, Company, Legal) untouched; RSS / JSON / llms.txt utility links and the copyright + Summers Solutions credit row untouched; W3C valid-RSS badge in the bottom row untouched; no rotating/spinning animations (lift + colour only); no "Follow us" or "Connect" sub-headings (the existing "Community" h3 covers it); icon-only deliberately not used here (footer column format makes the text label load-bearing for scannability — header is the icon-only surface). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings beyond the pre-existing `turbopack.root` notice. Local `next start` + curl on `/`: 6 distinct Community aria-labels rendered (View InnerZero on GitHub, Join InnerZero Discord community, Follow InnerZero on X / Instagram / LinkedIn, Support InnerZero on Ko-fi); the 4 brand-coloured hover classes (`hover:text-[#5865F2]`, `hover:text-[#E4405F]`, `hover:text-[#0A66C2]`, `hover:text-[#FF5E5B]`) all present in the compiled HTML; pre-existing labels gone (`>Support us<` returns 0, `>X (Twitter)<` returns 0); `https://github.com/zotex12/innerzero-releases` href count rose from 1 to 2 (header icon + new footer entry, exact expected delta); the "Community" h3 still renders so column ordering is visually identical; em-dash count unchanged at 1 (the pre-existing `Runs 100% locally — no cloud required` in `SoftwareApplication` JSON-LD, out of scope). | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |

Both open in new tabs. **New file `src/components/icons/GitHubIcon.tsx`:** named export, props interface with optional `className`, returns inline `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">` with the standard Octocat path and default `cn("w-5 h-5 shrink-0", className)` so it scales with parent text size and matches the Discord/Theme icons at h-9 w-9 button sizing. Same component shape as the existing `DiscordIcon.tsx` (which is reused as-is — no edit, just an inline `className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0"` override at the call site to bump it from its native w-4 h-4 SocialProofStrip default to the larger header size). **Header changes (`src/components/layout/Header.tsx`):** imported `DiscordIcon` + `GitHubIcon`, added two `<a>` elements before `<ThemeToggle />` inside the existing `<div className="flex items-center gap-2">` cluster. Each anchor exactly matches the ThemeToggle button class string for visual continuity: `flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg text-text-secondary transition-colors duration-150 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-gold cursor-pointer`. Hover diverges per brand: Discord uses `hover:text-[#5865F2]` (blurple, consistent with the SocialProofStrip pill); GitHub uses `hover:text-text-primary` (neutral brighten — GitHub has no primary brand colour that plays nicely with both light and dark modes). `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"` plus full `aria-label` (Join InnerZero Discord community / View InnerZero on GitHub) and `title` (Join Discord / View on GitHub) for hover tooltips. **Mobile fit:** the header right cluster at < md is logo + Discord + GitHub + ThemeToggle + hamburger trigger — `HeaderAuth` (Login + Download Free button) is already `hidden md:flex` so no overflow risk at 375px (~280px total cluster width vs ~343px usable). Discord and GitHub icons are deliberately NOT buried inside the hamburger; per spec they must remain visible at every viewport width because their discoverability matters more than the redundant in-header Download CTA (which already has presence in Hero, Footer, and the mobile nav drawer). **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; no text labels next to icons (icon-only per spec); no edit to the existing `DiscordIcon.tsx` (size override via prop only); no GitHub link to the private repo (only the public `innerzero-releases`); no star count, badge, or dynamic data; no new spacing or sizing values introduced (all match ThemeToggle's pattern); no reorder of existing header elements; no em dashes; footer Discord/GitHub additions explicitly out of scope (footer already has Discord in the Community column; GitHub-for-footer can be a later phase). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings. Local `next start` + curl: home page `<header>` contains both new anchors with the spec'd hrefs, aria-labels, target/rel attrs, title attrs, and the matching button class strings; DOM order is Discord-then-GitHub-then-ThemeToggle as intended; total Discord URL refs across the page rose from 2 to 3 (Footer column link + SocialProofStrip pill + new header icon — exactly the expected delta). | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **Download interstitial with community prompts** | New `/download/thanks` page sits between any platform-specific Download click and the actual GitHub asset URL. Auto-triggers the download ~500ms after page paint, then keeps the user on a clean centred layout with two voluntary community prompts (Star on GitHub, Join Discord) plus a footer pointing at the homepage FAQ and the support email. Ethical, voluntary, zero OAuth, zero pressure. **New file `src/app/(marketing)/download/thanks/page.tsx`:** server component (async). Reads `searchParams.asset` (Next 16 async API), then resolves the exact `browser_download_url` from the latest non-draft GitHub release whose `assets[].name` matches the requested filename. `next: { revalidate: 3600 }` on the GitHub fetch shares the same 1h ISR window as `/api/stats` so neither hot path hits the upstream API more than hourly. `GITHUB_TOKEN` attached as `Authorization: Bearer ...` when present (the Vercel env var added in the SocialProofStrip phase already covers this). 8s `AbortController` timeout. **Fault tolerance:** any fetch failure, abort, JSON-shape problem, or asset-name miss falls back to `https://github.com/zotex12/innerzero-releases/releases/latest`, so the user always lands somewhere useful — bookmarked stale links from prior versions resolve to the latest release page rather than a 404. **Page layout:** centred `max-w-2xl` single column. Hero: pulsing gold dot (CSS-only `animate-ping` plus a solid dot underneath, no JS), "Downloading InnerZero..." h1, "Your download should start automatically. If nothing happens, click here." paragraph with the manual fallback `<a href={resolvedUrl}>` carrying the exact same URL the AutoDownload island triggers (so a click on either route reaches the same byte stream). Two equal-weight community cards in a 2-col grid (1-col on mobile): GitHub card with inline Octocat SVG, headline "Star us on GitHub", subtext, "⭐ Star on GitHub" CTA pointing at the public repo with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`; Discord card with the existing `<DiscordIcon>`, headline "Join our Discord", "Join Discord" CTA pointing at `https://discord.gg/rn9SPXgThT`. Both cards lift on hover; GitHub card uses the gold border + gold-tinted shadow established by HeroScreenshots, Discord card uses the blurple border + blurple-tinted shadow established by SocialProofStrip's pill. Footer note: muted small text linking `/#faq` (the homepage FAQ section) and `mailto:help@innerzero.com`. **Metadata:** absolute title `"Downloading InnerZero..."` (bypasses the template), `noindex, follow` robots — transient interstitial, must not appear in search results, but crawlers should follow the outbound asset and community links so authority still flows. Canonical `/download/thanks`. **AutoDownload client island (`src/components/sections/AutoDownload.tsx`):** plain `"use client"` component, `useEffect` with 500ms `setTimeout` that creates a synthetic `<a>` element, sets `href` to the resolved asset URL, appends to body, calls `.click()`, removes from body. The 500ms delay lets the page paint before the OS download dialog appears so users see the community cards. Cleanup function clears the timeout to avoid double-fires on dev re-renders. Kept `target` unset so the browser handles it as a same-tab download (and the user lands back on the thank-you screen after the dialog dismisses). No `window.location.href` reassignment — that would risk navigating away from the thank-you page on browsers that interpret the URL change as a navigation rather than a download. **Updated entry points:** `src/app/(marketing)/download/DownloadCards.tsx` per-platform Download buttons now point at `/download/thanks?asset=<filename>` instead of the direct `https://github.com/...` URLs (Windows .exe, macOS .dmg, Linux .AppImage). The CLI `curl`/`wget` commands inside the collapsibles keep their direct GitHub URLs because terminal users want the raw target with no HTML interstitial. Other `/download` references on the site (Hero CTA, Header CTA, CTABanner, persona page CTAs, features-page CTA) all point at the platform-chooser page `/download` rather than at GitHub assets directly, so per spec scope they need no change — they already arrive at the chooser, then click through into the interstitial. JSON-LD `downloadUrl` and the `SoftwareApplication.offers.url` schema fields keep their direct GitHub asset URLs because schema consumers (Google, AI crawlers, package managers) want canonical asset endpoints, not interstitial redirects. **Anchor support:** `src/components/sections/HomeFAQ.tsx` section wrapper gained `id="faq"` and `scroll-mt-20` so `/#faq` links from the thanks page (and anywhere else) scroll cleanly past the fixed header. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; zero OAuth or GitHub-on-behalf actions; no pressuring copy ("helps more people discover InnerZero" / "get help, share feedback, meet other users" — both invitational); no whole-page auto-redirect away from the thank-you screen; no new tracking or analytics; em-dash audit clean; client island scoped to AutoDownload.tsx only — the page itself stays a server component; existing download flow preserved through the fallback to `releases/latest` on any error. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings; `/download/thanks` registered as a server-rendered route (`ƒ` — necessary because of `await searchParams`), with the underlying GitHub fetch ISR-cached 1h. Local `next start` + curl: with no asset query param the page renders the fallback `releases/latest` URL on both the manual click-here link and the AutoDownload anchor; with `?asset=InnerZero-Setup-0.1.4.exe` it resolves to the version-pinned `releases/download/v0.1.4/InnerZero-Setup-0.1.4.exe` URL; `<meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow">` present in head; `/download` platform-chooser page now renders all three card buttons routed through `/download/thanks?asset=...` (Windows .exe, macOS .dmg, Linux .AppImage); zero em dashes on the rendered thank-you page. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |

Both new placements use the static framing pattern (no lightbox, no client component overhead) since each is a single supporting image inside deep content rather than a gallery. **PART A — `/for/developers`:** widened the shared `PersonaPage` component with an additive optional `heroScreenshot?: PersonaScreenshot` prop (typed `{ src, alt, caption }`). When supplied, renders a section between the lead paragraphs and the "Why" block: outer dark frame `rounded-xl border border-border-default bg-[#0a0a0f] p-3 md:p-4`, inner `relative aspect-19/10 overflow-hidden rounded-md` wrapper, `<Image fill object-contain sizes="(max-width: 1024px) 100vw, 80vw">`, centred caption below. Pattern mirrors `HeroScreenshots` exactly so the visual language is consistent across surfaces. Only `developers/page.tsx` populates the new prop; the other four persona pages (writers, students, researchers, offline-work) are unchanged because the prop is optional and undefined. Developers page now passes `heroScreenshot: { src: "/images/appagentpageimage.png", alt: "InnerZero AI Specialists page showing the Coding specialist agent connected to Zero, with Automation and Art specialists marked coming soon", caption: "Zero's Coding Specialist handles code tasks in a separate model, hot-swapped in VRAM, with approval gates before any file changes." }`. The `modifiedDate` on the Article schema for that page bumped from `2026-04-19` to `2026-04-20` to reflect the content change. **PART B — `/features`:** added `Fragment` import and an `Image` import to `src/app/(marketing)/features/page.tsx`. Switched the `SECTIONS.map` callback from returning a bare `<section>` to returning a `<Fragment key={...}>` containing the section AND a conditional screenshot section that renders only when `section.title === "Customisation"`. The injected section uses the same dark-frame + 19:10 aspect pattern as Part A, with `max-w-5xl mx-auto` for a slightly wider single-image surface than the persona article (which is `max-w-4xl`). Inherits the parent Customisation section's `bg` value so the alternating `bg-bg-secondary` rhythm of the features page is preserved. Caption: "Choose a prebuilt personality or write your own. Applies to both text chat and voice." Alt: "InnerZero personality settings with Professional, Friendly, and Concise presets plus a custom personality creation form". Visually pairs with the existing Customisation feature card "AI personality" which already mentions presets + custom personalities. **Asset commit:** `public/images/appagentpageimage.png` and `public/images/appsettingspersonalitypage.png` (both ~128-153 KB, untracked since the original asset upload) are added in the same commit so Vercel does not 404 on the image URLs the rendered HTML references. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; zero changes to `HeroScreenshots`, `HomeFAQ`, or any homepage component; zero changes to the four other persona pages; the `appchat` / `appvoice` / `appprivacy` PNGs that already live on the homepage grid are NOT reused on these pages; existing features and persona copy is unchanged beyond the additive prop / fragment injection; no em dashes anywhere in the new copy or the new schema. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings; both `/features` and `/for/developers` registered as static (`○`). Local `next start` + curl: `/for/developers` renders the appagent screenshot via Next/Image optimisation (`/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fappagentpageimage.png&w=3840&q=75`) with the spec'd alt text; `/features` renders the appsettings screenshot via the same optimised pipeline; neither page contains any `aria-modal` markup so the lightbox interaction is correctly absent (lightbox stays homepage-only); zero em dashes on either rendered page. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **Homepage FAQ section + FAQPage JSON-LD** | Curated 5-question FAQ between PrivacyStatement and CTABanner on the homepage, with FAQPage structured data for AEO + SEO lift. **Inventory taken first per spec:** existing `src/components/sections/FAQ.tsx` is hardcoded to the site-wide `FAQ_DATA` array and is itself a client component with React state and ScrollReveal. It takes no data prop, so reusing it would either dump the full pricing FAQ on the homepage (off spec) or require widening its API just for this surface. **Path C** picked: built a fresh server component using native `<details>/<summary>` disclosure with zero JS, sidestepping the existing component entirely. The pricing-page FAQ is intentionally untouched. **New file `src/components/sections/HomeFAQ.tsx`:** typed `HomeFAQItem` interface, exported `HOME_FAQ` data array (5 items), and the `<HomeFAQ />` server component. Exporting the data alongside the component lets the homepage page.tsx build matching FAQPage JSON-LD from the same source array, so the schema text matches the displayed copy byte-for-byte (zero risk of drift) — Google treats mismatched schema as manipulative, so single-source-of-truth here is non-negotiable. Inline ChevronDown SVG (no lucide import) rotates 180° on `group-open:rotate-180`. **Item structure:** `<details className="group rounded-xl border border-border-default bg-bg-card px-6 py-4 transition-colors hover:border-border-hover [[open]]:border-accent-gold/40">` — gold-tinted border on the open state via Tailwind's canonical bracket-attr selector (Tailwind v4 form, no `&` prefix needed since the current element is implicit). `<summary>` strips the native marker (`[&::-webkit-details-marker]:hidden`, `list-none`), keeps the cursor pointer, and carries its own focus ring (`focus-visible:ring-accent-gold ring-offset-bg-primary`). Native keyboard behaviour (Enter / Space toggle) is preserved by NOT overriding it. Centred container `max-w-3xl mx-auto`, items spaced `space-y-3`. Section wrapper `py-20 md:py-28` matches the rhythm of the surrounding PrivacyStatement and CTABanner sections. Section header uses the existing `<SectionHeader>` primitive with title "Questions, answered" and subtitle "Everything you need to know before you download." **5 Q&A pairs (verbatim from spec):** "Is InnerZero really free?" / "Does my data leave my machine?" / "What platforms does it run on?" / "Do I need a powerful PC?" / "Can I use Claude, GPT, or Gemini with InnerZero?" Last answer carries the literal £ symbol ("£9.99 per month") in both the visible copy and the JSON-LD payload. **Homepage mount (`src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx`):** `<HomeFAQ />` placed between `<PrivacyStatement />` and `<CTABanner />`. Existing JSON-LD inline script tag (Organization + SoftwareApplication + WebSite) is left exactly as-is; a separate `<JsonLd>` component call below it emits the FAQPage schema, built from `HOME_FAQ.map(...)` so the schema array reflects whatever is in the source data. Two adjacent script tags is fine — Google parses each independently, and keeping the FAQPage in its own tag avoids accidentally tangling it with the existing schema graph if either changes. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; no client component (native `<details>` is server-renderable); pricing-page FAQ untouched (verified — `src/app/(marketing)/pricing/page.tsx` and `src/components/sections/PricingSection.tsx` not in this commit's diff); no Hero / HeroScreenshots / SocialProofStrip / FeatureCards / HowItWorks / PrivacyStatement / CTABanner changes; no images or icons inside answer text; no em dashes in any new copy (the single em dash in the rendered HTML lives in the pre-existing SoftwareApplication JSON-LD `featureList`, out of scope); no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` outside the existing JsonLd helper. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero new warnings beyond the pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace inference notice. Local `next start` + curl on `/`: 5 `<details>` elements rendered, 5 `<summary>` elements, "Questions, answered" h2 present once, exactly 1 `"@type":"FAQPage"` schema with 5 `"@type":"Question"` entries, the literal £ symbol present in both visible copy and JSON-LD `acceptedAnswer.text`. Spot-checked Q5: visible answer string and schema `text` field are character-for-character identical including the £9.99 substring. Schema is in the initial server-rendered HTML (not client-hydrated); `view-source` would surface the same `<script type="application/ld+json">` blocks. | COMPLETE 2026-04-20 |
| **HeroScreenshots polish: lightbox, frame theming, gap, crop fix** | Four targeted fixes to the freshly shipped HeroScreenshots component. **(1) Click behaviour split:** converted from server to client component (`"use client"` at the top) to host lightbox state. The card is now an `<article>` containing a sibling `<button>` (image area + label block) and an absolutely-positioned `<Link href="/download">` in the bottom-right. Clicking the image opens a full-screen lightbox; clicking the "Download free →" link routes to /download. The link sits as a sibling of the button (not nested inside) and stops click propagation as a belt-and-braces guard, but the absolute positioning + `z-10` + sibling-not-child structure makes the propagation path unreachable in the first place. Both keep their own `focus-visible:ring-accent-gold` so keyboard users see distinct focus targets. **Lightbox:** `useState<number | null>` for `openIndex`. Three `useEffect`s, each correctly cleaning up: a focus-management effect (focus close button on open, return focus to the originating card button on close via a `triggerRefs` array + `prevOpenIndex` tracker), an Escape-key listener (only attached while open), and a body-scroll lock that captures and restores the prior `document.body.style.overflow` value rather than blanking it. Overlay is `role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Enlarged screenshot"`, fixed `inset-0 z-50` with `bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-sm`. Close interactions: dedicated 40x40 close button (top-right, with its own focus ring offset by `ring-offset-black` since it sits on the dark backdrop), backdrop click, and Escape. The image is in a nested div that calls `e.stopPropagation()` so clicking the image itself does NOT close the lightbox — only the surrounding backdrop does. Caption shows the label + sublabel below the image. No keyboard arrow navigation between images per spec scope. No new npm packages — pure React state + plain CSS transitions. **(2) Crop fix:** read actual source dims first (`appchatpageimage.png` 2558x1350, `appvoicepageimage.png` 2555x1339, `appprivacypage.png` 2555x1349) — all three are within 1% of 19:10. The previous `width={2558} height={1350}` props treated all three as identical 19:10 even though two are slightly off, and were rendering with `w-full h-auto` which doesn't crop but does force the wrapper to inherit whatever Next/Image computed. Replaced with: outer dark frame `relative overflow-hidden rounded-t-xl bg-[#0a0a0f] p-3 md:p-4`, inner aspect wrapper `relative aspect-19/10 overflow-hidden rounded-md`, and `<Image fill object-contain>` inside that wrapper. With a 19:10 box and a near-19:10 source, `object-contain` fits the full app UI cleanly with imperceptible vertical letterboxing on the two off-by-1% files (a few px at most). The full sidebar, nav, and content of every screenshot is visible — no slice cropping. Used canonical `aspect-19/10` per the linter's `suggestCanonicalClasses` hint (Tailwind v4 syntax) instead of the arbitrary-value form. `sizes` and `priority` props preserved per LCP guidance. **(3) Theming fix:** the dark `#0a0a0f` frame is hard-coded so it stays dark in both modes, matching the screenshot's own UI for a seamless visual transition (the screenshot's window edge becomes the frame's inner edge). Card body retains `bg-bg-card` + `border-border-default` so the label block adapts: cream/light in light mode, dark in dark mode. The `border-t border-border-default` between the dark frame and the light label block creates the "framed product shot" effect cleanly in light mode and a subtle separator in dark mode. Hover ramp (gold border, `-translate-y-1`, gold-tinted shadow, image scale `1.02`) preserved verbatim from the previous phase. **(4) Gap fix:** section margin `mt-10 md:mt-14` shrunk to `mt-0 md:mt-2`. Hero already provides bottom padding; the previous double-spacing was creating a visible void between the Discord pill and the first card. Bottom margin (`mb-20 md:mb-28`) preserved so the next section breathes. **Scope guards held:** zero changes to `Hero.tsx`, `SocialProofStrip.tsx`, `FeatureCards.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, or any other component; zero new npm packages (no Yet Another Lightbox, no headlessui, no radix, no Framer); image file paths and alt text unchanged; "Download free →" link still routes to `/download`; no keyboard arrow navigation between lightbox images (out of scope per task); no em dashes in customer-facing text (lightbox caption uses ". " separator after the bold label rather than a dash). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean; zero new warnings. Local `next start` + curl: home page renders 3 `aspect-19/10` wrappers, 3 distinct `aria-label="Enlarge ..."` button labels (Private Chat / Voice Mode / Your Privacy, Visible), 3 dark-frame `bg-[#0a0a0f]` containers, 7 total `href="/download"` matches across the page (Header CTA + 3 card links + Footer + JSON-LD references — expected). The single em dash in the rendered HTML lives in the pre-existing home-page `SoftwareApplication` JSON-LD (`"Runs 100% locally — no cloud required"`) and is out of scope for this phase. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Hero product screenshots showcase + RSS validator badge** | Two complementary visual upgrades. **PART A — Hero screenshots:** new `src/components/sections/HeroScreenshots.tsx` server component renders a 3-column grid (1 col mobile, 3 col `md:` and up) of dark-themed product screenshots immediately under the hero, mounted between `<Hero />` and `<FeatureCards />` in `src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx`. The route file lives in the marketing route group, NOT bare `src/app/page.tsx` as the spec text described — adjusted to actual site structure.
