Anchor goes to `http://validator.w3.org/feed/check.cgi?url=https%3A//innerzero.com/feed.xml` with `target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"`, full `aria-label="Validate InnerZero RSS feed with W3C"`, and `title` for hover tooltip. Image is local at `/images/valid-rss.png` (88x31, the standard W3C valid-RSS asset, already present in `public/images/`), rendered via Next/Image at `h-6 w-auto` with `opacity-70` resting state ramping to `opacity-100` on hover so the badge nods at attention without stealing it. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages (no Framer, no GSAP — CSS transitions + the existing ScrollReveal primitive only); zero changes to Hero.tsx, SocialProofStrip, FeatureCards, HowItWorks, PrivacyStatement, or CTABanner; no hotlinking of the W3C image; no more than 3 screenshots; no browser-chrome mockups around the screenshots; no em dashes; no "View all features" link (each screenshot IS the CTA per spec); no `loading="lazy"` on the first screenshot (priority=true for LCP); sublabel copy stays one short line, not a paragraph. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean; zero new warnings beyond the pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace inference notice. Local `next start` + curl: home page renders all three Next-optimised image URLs (`/_next/image?url=%2Fimages%2Fapp...&w=3840&q=75`), three correct `aria-label`s on the screenshot anchors (Private Chat / Voice Mode / Your Privacy, Visible), the W3C validator anchor with the exact spec'd href in the footer, the local `valid-rss.png` returns HTTP 200 from `/images/valid-rss.png`, and three `Download free →` affordance spans rendered (one per card). Hero h1 ("Your AI. Your machine. Your data.") and the `data-testid="social-proof-strip"` strip remain byte-for-byte unchanged. **Asset note:** `/public/images/valid-rss.png` was already present from a prior phase, so the badge renders immediately on this commit. If the file ever goes missing, Next/Image falls back to a 404 at runtime with no build break. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Discord logo pill in social proof strip** | Visual upgrade to the homepage SocialProofStrip Discord link: replaces the plain-text "Join our Discord →" anchor with a pill-style container that pairs the official Discord brandmark SVG with the link text. Goal: make the community CTA visually distinct from the muted downloads/version stats without adding noise or animation, and give the brandmark a moment in the visual hierarchy where it earns recognition for users who scan rather than read. **New file `src/components/icons/DiscordIcon.tsx`:** named export, props interface with optional `className`, returns an inline `<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true">` with the official single-path Discord brandmark and default `cn("w-4 h-4 shrink-0", className)` so it scales by parent text size and matches the strip's `text-sm` height. The mark is the standard discord.com/branding brandmark, public asset explicitly licensed for use when linking to a Discord server (which is exactly what this anchor does). `currentColor` fill means the icon picks up the link's text colour, so it transitions from muted secondary to the official Discord blurple `#5865F2` on hover alongside the rest of the pill. **Strip changes (`src/components/sections/SocialProofStrip.tsx`):** items array now carries an optional `noBullet?: boolean` per entry; the bullet-rendering condition becomes `i > 0 && !item.noBullet` so the Discord pill (which sets `noBullet: true`) renders without a leading bullet, while the downloads-version bullet stays put. Pill anchor classes: `inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-border-default bg-bg-card/40 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary no-underline transition-colors duration-150` for the resting state; hover ramp `hover:border-[#5865F2] hover:bg-[#5865F2]/10 hover:text-[#5865F2]`; keyboard focus `focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[#5865F2] focus-visible:ring-offset-2 focus-visible:ring-offset-bg-primary`. Trailing arrow character removed (the pill border is sufficient affordance). Existing `href`, `target`, `rel`, and `aria-label` attributes preserved verbatim. Uses canonical Tailwind tokens for design-system colours (`text-text-secondary`, `border-border-default`, `bg-bg-card/40`, `ring-offset-bg-primary`) and arbitrary-value classes only for the Discord brand blurple `#5865F2`, since blurple has no token in the InnerZero palette and should not get one (it is foreign brand colour applied only to the Discord pill in interactive states). **Scope guards held:** zero changes to the downloads stat or version pill text/styling; container layout unchanged beyond the one bullet removal before Discord; `Hero.tsx` untouched; no new npm packages; no `lucide-react` (Discord mark is not in their icon set, hence the inline SVG component); no em dashes anywhere. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean, zero TypeScript errors, zero new warnings beyond the pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace inference notice. Local `next start` + curl: home-page HTML renders the strip with downloads → bullet → version → pill (no leading bullet on the pill); pill anchor carries all spec'd Tailwind classes including the blurple hover and focus-ring tokens; inline SVG path is byte-identical to the spec'd Discord brandmark. Light/dark theme handling covered by canonical tokens (`border-border-default` and `bg-bg-card` swap with theme); blurple stays constant since it is brand colour, not theme colour. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Live download counter + Discord hero strip** | New homepage social-proof strip below the existing CTA buttons, surfacing aggregate GitHub-release download count, latest version, and a Discord community link. Goal: cheap social proof on the highest-traffic surface without animations, tick-up counters, or any other visual noise; honest numbers only, sourced live from the public release repo. **New endpoint `src/app/api/stats/route.ts`:** GET handler with `revalidate = 3600` (1h ISR) returning a fully-typed `StatsResponse` `{ ok, total, rounded, release_count, latest_version, latest_published_at }`. Wraps `getStatsPayload()` from the shared helper module. **New helper `src/lib/stats.ts`:** owns the GitHub fetch, aggregation, and rounding rules. Hits `https://api.github.com/repos/zotex12/innerzero-releases/releases?per_page=100` with an `AbortController`-backed 8-second timeout. Sums `asset.download_count` across every asset of every non-draft release (drafts skipped explicitly so unpublished installers do not pad the count). Picks `latest_version` and `latest_published_at` by maximum `Date.parse(published_at)`. Headers include `Accept: application/vnd.github+json` and the `X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28` pin so an upstream API revision does not silently change the response shape on us. `GITHUB_TOKEN` env var attached as `Authorization: Bearer ...` when present (5000/hr rate limit); when missing, `console.warn` once per fetch and fall back to the unauthenticated 60/hr-per-IP path. **Rounding rules** per spec: `total < 100` returns `null` so the UI suppresses the line entirely, `100..9999` rounds down to nearest 100 with a `+` suffix (`507 -> "500+"`, `1203 -> "1200+"`), `>= 10000` rounds down to nearest 1000 in K-units (`12050 -> "12K+"`, `150000 -> "150K+"`). **Fault tolerance:** any fetch error, abort, or non-200 response is caught and converted to the `EMPTY` payload (`{ ok: false, total: 0, rounded: null, release_count: 0, latest_version: null, latest_published_at: null }`); the route handler always returns HTTP 200, the helper always resolves, never throws. **New component `src/components/sections/SocialProofStrip.tsx`:** server component (no `"use client"`). Imports `getStatsPayload` directly rather than self-fetching `/api/stats` over HTTP — that pattern was tried first but failed because the strip would prerender against `https://innerzero.com/api/stats` (a route that does not exist on the live site mid-deploy), silently catch the 404, and render nothing. Direct import avoids the self-hop entirely while preserving the public `/api/stats` endpoint for browser inspection and external consumers. Renders a `flex flex-wrap` row with "Rounded downloads", "vX.Y.Z live", and "Join our Discord →" separated by muted bullets; each item is a sibling so the wrap behaviour at 375px naturally stacks neighbouring items rather than mid-item. Discord link is a real `<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="Join InnerZero Discord community">` to `https://discord.gg/rn9SPXgThT`; everything else is plain text. Hidden states: `rounded === null` hides the downloads span, `latest_version === null` hides the version pill, and `(!ok && total === 0)` returns null entirely so the hero is never cluttered with a bullet-only Discord row. Outer element gets `data-testid="social-proof-strip"` for test harnesses. Uses canonical Tailwind tokens (`text-text-secondary`, `text-text-muted`, `hover:text-accent-teal`) per the linter's `suggestCanonicalClasses` hint and the codebase convention; the spec's literal `text-[color:var(--text-secondary)]` form would have produced identical CSS but introduced a stylistic inconsistency with every other component on the site. **Hero mount (`src/components/sections/Hero.tsx`):** import added; `<SocialProofStrip className="mt-8" />` placed as a sibling immediately after the existing CTA wrapper div (NOT nested inside it). H1, tagline, description paragraph, both CTAs, the radial gradient glow, and the floating particles are all byte-for-byte unchanged. **Environment variable:** `GITHUB_TOKEN` should be added to Vercel env vars for Production + Preview + Development. A fine-grained PAT with `public_repo` read scope on `zotex12/innerzero-releases` is sufficient. The site works without it but hits the unauthenticated 60/hr-per-IP rate limit, which would degrade to the empty payload (and therefore null strip) under sustained traffic — acceptable but not ideal. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; zero changes to the H1, tagline, description, CTAs, or hero background; no client-side GitHub API calls; no animation, tick-up counter, pulsing dot, or visual gimmick; no hardcoded download numbers anywhere in the codebase; no per-platform / per-version breakdown in the strip; no em dashes; no charts or graphs; no `/stats` page; no Discord member-count integration. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean; `/api/stats` registered as static (`○`) with the 1h ISR annotation. Local `next start` + curl: `/api/stats` returns the expected JSON shape (`{"ok":true,"total":507,"rounded":"500+","release_count":4,"latest_version":"v0.1.4","latest_published_at":"2026-04-18T..."}`, matching the dashboard sanity check); home-page HTML renders the strip with `data-testid="social-proof-strip"`, `500+ downloads`, `v0.1.4 live`, bullet separators, and the Discord anchor with the `→` arrow; H1 still reads "Your AI. Your machine. Your data."; CTA wrapper class string identical to pre-change. Null-fallback path verified by code inspection: `getStatsPayload` never throws; on any failure path returns EMPTY which trips the `(!ok && total === 0)` early-return in the strip → no broken layout, no empty bullets. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Persona landing pages + pillar cross-link + Use cases footer** | Five `/for/*` persona landing pages to catch middle-of-funnel "AI for X" queries that neither the definitional pillar (`/what-is-local-ai`) nor the entity reference (`/models`) targets directly. Thesis: someone Googling "AI for developers" is further down the buying intent curve than someone Googling "what is local AI"; give them a page that maps their specific pain to specific InnerZero features, with honest trade-offs, no persona-specific feature list duplication, and clean schema so answer engines can cite persona-to-feature mappings. **Shared component:** `src/app/for/PersonaPage.tsx` — one server-component layout taking a typed `PersonaPageProps` (slug, persona, h1, leadPain, leadFit, 3-5 `PersonaBullet`s, 3 `PersonaHowCard`s with `React.ReactNode` bodies so each card can embed its own inline blog links, 4-6 `PersonaFAQ` pairs, 3 `PersonaRelatedPost`s, publishedDate, modifiedDate). Renders: hero (h1 + two-sentence lead: pain then honest fit), "Why [persona] choose local AI" bullet list with accent-teal dots, "How InnerZero helps [persona]" 3-card grid (`grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3`), "Frequently asked questions" divide-y list, "Further reading" panel with the 3 per-persona blog posts plus `/what-is-local-ai` and `/models`, and a CTA section with Download / See features / See pricing. Emits Article + FAQPage JSON-LD as a two-element array via the existing shared `<JsonLd>` helper (auto-escapes quotes, apostrophes, Unicode via JSON.stringify). Explicit-over-clever: JSX for the how-card bodies lives in each persona file so copy edits touch one file, not a shared rewrite. **Five pages (each ~80 lines = typed `PAGE_DATA` constant + 3-line default export calling `<PersonaPage />`):** `/for/developers` targets "code never leaves disk", local coding models (Qwen 3 8B/30B range), sandbox-first agent, BYO frontier keys for Claude Opus 4.7 / GPT-5 / Gemini 2.5 Pro, offline-on-planes — FAQs on privacy / Opus coding use / local coding models / editor integration / sandbox safety. `/for/writers` targets persistent project memory, local voice dictation via faster-whisper, tuneable personality, no deadline paywall — FAQs on training / dictation / model choice / cloud structural editing / memory persistence. `/for/students` targets free-forever, no email, off-university-network, offline study, laptop-friendly — 6 FAQs on trial-vs-free / network privacy / coursework policy / internet dependency / citation / no-GPU fallback. `/for/researchers` targets no third-party retention, frontier via BYO for heavy synthesis, project-scoped memory, local doc Q&A, connection log auditability — FAQs on retention / BYO privacy model / honest compare-vs-ChatGPT for lit review / connection log / coding-agent data isolation. `/for/offline-work` targets full-offline-after-setup, knowledge packs for factual grounding, remote Ollama over LAN, strictly-opt-in cloud — FAQs on what works offline / one-time internet needs / plane use / offline voice / hallucination mitigation. Each persona's bullet list is distinct from the others (content audit: every bullet was rewritten if it read as generic; writers get "persistent project memory", developers get "code never leaves your disk", and they are not the same bullet with different words). **Pillar cross-link:** `/what-is-local-ai` gained a "Who is local AI for?" section between "How does InnerZero fit?" and the FAQ, with a 2-column card grid (5 cards, one per persona, each showing the persona name + a one-line pitch + link). ToC item inserted. No other pillar content changed. **Footer:** `src/lib/constants.ts` `FOOTER_COLUMNS` gained a fifth entry "Use cases" inserted between Product and Company per spec, with 5 persona links. `src/components/layout/Footer.tsx` grid widened from `md:grid-cols-5` to `md:grid-cols-6` to accommodate the new column (Brand col-span-1 + 5 link columns = 6 slots); mobile layout unchanged (`grid-cols-2` + Brand col-span-2 is identical). Header and MobileNav untouched — footer-only discipline matches `/models` and `/what-is-local-ai`, so Louie can review all five pages live on Vercel before deciding whether to promote any to main nav. **Scope guards held:** zero new npm packages; zero changes to `/features`, `/pricing`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/models` page bodies (diff confirms only `what-is-local-ai/page.tsx`, `Footer.tsx`, `constants.ts`, and the new `for/` tree changed); zero pricing figures or tier names duplicated on the persona pages (they reference `/pricing` instead); zero feature-list duplication (each persona picks 3-5 relevant features and links out to `/features` for the rest); no invented stats, no benchmark numbers, no em dashes, no banned AI-writing phrases (grep audit clean); honest trade-offs in the researcher page (explicit "frontier cloud models still lead on hardest-case synthesis" line pointing to BYO as the honest escape hatch). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean; all five `/for/*` routes and the updated `/what-is-local-ai` register as static (`○`). `next start` + curl per persona: 200 OK, correct h1 ("AI for developers" / "AI for writers" / "AI for students" / "AI for researchers" / "AI for offline work"), 5 h2s (Why / How / FAQ / Further reading / CTA's "Ready to try it?"), 1 Article schema + 1 FAQPage schema with 5 or 6 Question entries (matches declared FAQ length), zero em dashes. Pillar page renders all 5 `/for/*` hrefs in the new persona grid and 12 anchor ids (the 11 pre-existing plus `who-is-local-ai-for`). Home-page footer renders the "Use cases" label and all 5 `/for/*` hrefs; `md:grid-cols-6` class present exactly where expected. `git diff --name-only` shows only the four intended paths changed. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Blog AEO pass: question h2s + topical link fanout** | Minimal-touch edit sweep across all existing blog posts plus the auto-published Claude Opus BYO post to make each one more citable by answer engines and to push topical authority into the canonical hubs (`/models`, `/blog/ai-that-remembers`, `/blog/ollama-desktop-app`, `/blog/knowledge-packs-explained`, `/blog/claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys`, `/blog/innerzero-vs-lm-studio`). **Method:** one Node script defined a per-file H2_REWRITES map (old exact line -> new exact line) and a MODEL_LINKS + PHRASE_LINKS ruleset; ran once, deleted. Script masked existing `[label](url)` spans with null-byte fill before phrase-search so no new link could ever land inside an existing markdown link. Each file enforced a 5-link cap across both models and hub phrases, skipped self-referential link targets (e.g. ollama-desktop-app.mdx never links "Ollama" to itself), and respected word-boundary regex on every phrase. Frontmatter was not read beyond detecting `draft: true` and future-dated posts (neither present today). **H2 rewrites — 92 conversions from statement/noun-phrase to natural user question across the corpus:** every rewrite was hand-authored into the H2_REWRITES map rather than algorithmically generated, so phrasing stays idiomatic (e.g. "Memory architecture" -> "How does InnerZero's memory work?", "Privacy" -> "Are knowledge packs private?", "RAM: 16 GB minimum, 32 GB ideal" -> "How much RAM do I need for local AI?"). 64 h2s deliberately left as-is: CTA labels ("Try it", "Get started", "Download InnerZero"), listicle numbered items (things-you-can-do post), catalog product names in comparison posts (Ollama / LM Studio / GPT4All / Jan / Msty / Chatbox / etc. as category h2s), narrative beats ("The problem", "The idea", "The mission"), and 16 h2s that already read as questions before this pass (mostly in claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys which shipped Q-form from the start, plus the four already-Q h2s in what-is-a-local-ai-assistant, voice-mode, and what-models posts). **Post-run audit caught five h2 link bleeds** where the link-insertion pass added an internal link inside a heading line (e.g. `## How does [Ollama](/blog/ollama-desktop-app) fit in?`). Each was fixed manually: heading unwrapped to plain text, link moved to the first paragraph below so the h2 stays clean for anchor-id generation and schema extraction. Specifically: two in best-free-ai-windows-2026 (Ollama and LM Studio catalog headings both had links stripped because the post is a comparison piece whose body already references both products extensively), plus one memory link moved in how-innerzero-stays-private, one Ollama link moved in open-source-ai-models-explained, and one Ollama link moved in what-models-does-innerzero-use. **Topical link fanout — roughly 55 new internal links added across the corpus (~2.2 per post average, well under the 5-per-post cap):** diff-counted net-new targets were `/blog/ai-that-remembers` x20 (memory hub — the phrase "memory" appears in almost every post), `/blog/ollama-desktop-app` x12, `/blog/knowledge-packs-explained` x7, `/models` x6, `/blog/claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys` x6 (on "bring your own keys" / "your own API key" phrasing), `/download` x2, `/blog/innerzero-vs-lm-studio` x2. Models linked individually to `/models`: Claude Opus 4.7, Qwen 3, DeepSeek, etc. — one link per model per post per the rule, not aggregated through a single "supported models" link. Every addition was verified against the word-boundary regex and the link-span mask so no phrase was double-linked and no existing link was disturbed. **claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys.mdx** (CRLF-terminated by the auto-publish script, which broke the main script's frontmatter regex) was handled with three manual Edits: Claude Opus 4.7 -> `/models`, LM Studio -> `/blog/innerzero-vs-lm-studio`, memory -> `/blog/ai-that-remembers`; that post's h2s were already all Q-form so no rewrites needed. **Scope guards held:** zero frontmatter edits (titles, slugs, dates, tags, descriptions, readingTime all preserved — diff confirms); zero new files or posts; zero new h2s added (this is an edit pass, not a writing pass); `blog-queue/` at repo root completely untouched; `/pricing`, `/features`, `/privacy`, `/terms`, `/changelog` untouched (diff confirms only `src/content/blog/**/*.mdx` + this WEBCLAUDE log modified); first sentences left as-is where they already led with substance (no windup rewrites were needed on today's inventory — the corpus has been opinionated-voice-first since launch); zero em dashes introduced anywhere; no banned AI-writing phrases added. **Prompt A link preservation:** `grep -c "/what-is-local-ai" src/content/blog/*.mdx` returns exactly 14 files with exactly 14 refs total, matching Prompt A's insertion set byte-for-byte; no pillar link was overwritten or duplicated during this pass (the link-span mask in the script guaranteed that). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean (MDX compilation is strict about malformed link syntax; zero errors means every `[label](url)` parses). Local `next start` + curl on a five-post spot-check (what-models-does-innerzero-use, hardware-for-local-ai, voice-mode-innerzero, knowledge-packs-explained, use-ai-offline) returns 200 for each with 5-7 rendered question-form h2s apiece. No inline `<a>` tag appears inside any rendered `<h2>` element (verified with a targeted curl+grep). `git diff --name-only` shows only `src/content/blog/**` and this file changed — pricing / features / privacy / terms / changelog / layout / components / lib / public all untouched, as required. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **/what-is-local-ai pillar page + FAQ schema audit** | New AEO pillar targeting the "local AI" category head term (InnerZero's own category, where it has a fighting chance of ranking long-tail, rather than the "private AI" term dominated by HPE / Cloudera / AI21). Single authoritative reference for "What is local AI?", optimised for both traditional SEO (every h2 is a real-search question, answered in a 2-3 sentence chunk first then the detail) and AEO (FAQPage schema + Article schema so LLMs and rich-result crawlers can extract clean Q&A pairs). **New file:** `src/app/what-is-local-ai/page.tsx` — server component, canonical `/what-is-local-ai`, absolute title `"What is Local AI? - InnerZero"` (bypasses the `%s | InnerZero...` template), 134-char meta description. **Page structure:** hero (h1 "What is local AI?" matching the head query word-for-word + one-sentence definition lead); 11-item table of contents in a `<nav><ol>` with lowercase-kebab anchor links; ten question-style h2s in order (definition / how it works / vs cloud AI / hardware / free / private / offline / models / capabilities / how InnerZero fits), each with a dedicated `Answer` component for the direct 2-3 sentence snippet chunk then `Body` paragraphs for detail; a responsive `overflow-x-auto` 4x3 comparison table (Privacy / Cost / Performance / Capability rows x Local AI / Cloud AI / Hybrid columns) with honest trade-offs (cloud wins capability on frontier reasoning; local wins privacy, cost, offline); an 8-item FAQ section (Do I need a GPU / cost / vs ChatGPT / own API key / what is Ollama / what is LM Studio / sensitive documents / Mac Linux); a Further reading panel linking six relevant blog posts. **JSON-LD:** two schemas on the page via the shared `<JsonLd>` helper — `Article` (headline, description, author Louie, publisher InnerZero with logo image, datePublished + dateModified 2026-04-19, mainEntityOfPage, image og-default.png) and `FAQPage` (mainEntity array built from the 8 FAQ Q&A pairs only; the h2 questions deliberately stay out of the FAQPage payload per the task's schema discipline — they are navigational, not snippet-formatted). Schema object passed to `JsonLd` as an array so both get emitted via `JSON.stringify` (properly escapes quotes, apostrophes, and Unicode automatically). **FAQPage schema audit (pre-existing surfaces):** `/pricing` already emits FAQPage built from `[...FAQ_DATA, ...CLOUD_FAQ]`; `/privacy` already emits FAQPage built from its local `FAQ_ITEMS`; `src/components/sections/FAQ.tsx` exists but is not imported anywhere in the marketing tree (`grep "sections/FAQ"` returns zero hits) so there is no un-schemad visible FAQ on the home page. No backfill required; pillar page adds a third FAQPage to the site's schema coverage. **Footer link:** `src/lib/constants.ts` `FOOTER_COLUMNS.Product` now reads Features / What is local AI? / Models / Pricing / Download / Changelog (inserted between Features and Models per spec). Header nav and MobileNav untouched — footer-only discipline matches the /models rollout so Louie can review the live page before promoting. **Internal-link fanout from blog (one link per post, first body mention, skip-if-already-linked rule):** automated pass via a single Node script that strips frontmatter, masks existing markdown link spans with null-byte fill so the phrase search can never land inside a `[label](url)` tail, finds the first case-insensitive `\blocal ai\b` in unmasked territory, and wraps just that one occurrence in `[original-casing](/what-is-local-ai)`. Post-rewrite idempotence check confirms exactly one `/what-is-local-ai` href per touched file. **Results:** 14 posts updated (ai-that-remembers, best-free-ai-windows-2026, hardware-for-local-ai, innerzero-is-live, innerzero-vs-gpt4all, innerzero-vs-jan, innerzero-vs-lm-studio, local-ai-vs-cloud-ai, ollama-desktop-app, run-ai-on-your-pc, voice-mode-innerzero, what-is-a-local-ai-assistant, what-models-does-innerzero-use, why-we-built-innerzero); 5 posts skipped because they already contained a markdown link with "local AI" in its visible text pointing elsewhere (ai-without-subscription, claude-opus-4-7-byo-keys, how-innerzero-stays-private, use-ai-offline, why-your-ai-should-remember-you) — those existing links stay, no double-linking; 7 posts had no body mention (only tag metadata) so were correctly untouched. 14 + 5 + 7 = 26, matches the full post count. **Scope guards held:** no new npm packages; no changes to Features / Pricing / Privacy page copy (pillar links out, doesn't duplicate); no invented pricing, benchmarks, or model capabilities (all claims pulled from `install_profile.py` tier table via the /models data and existing privacy statements); no change to MDX frontmatter schema; zero edits to `/public/llms.txt` or `/public/llms-full.txt` (separate pass); no nav or MobileNav additions; zero em dashes (searched and confirmed); no banned AI-writing phrases (dive into / seamless / revolutionary / unlock / leverage / In conclusion / Furthermore / Moreover — all absent from the page copy). Responsive table: `overflow-x-auto` wrapper with `min-w-[640px]` inner table so at 375px the table horizontal-scrolls within its container rather than busting layout. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean with `/what-is-local-ai` registered as static route (`○`). Local `next start` + curl confirms 200 OK, single h1, eleven h2 ids (10 content + faq), both JSON-LD blocks present and parseable (one Article, one FAQPage with 8 Question entries), og-default.png referenced absolutely. Grep of `src/content/blog/**/*.mdx` for `/what-is-local-ai` returns exactly 14 matches, one per touched post. Expected on next Rich Results Test after Vercel deploy: Article + FAQPage both detected on `/what-is-local-ai`, FAQPage still detected on `/pricing` and `/privacy` (unchanged). | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **SEO canonical: flip root convention to no-slash** | Follow-up correction to the prior canonical phase. Next.js 14+ auto-strips the trailing slash from `<link rel="canonical">` and `<meta property="og:url">` for the root URL regardless of how `alternates.canonical` or `metadataBase` is configured (Vercel-confirmed in discussion #65323; tracked issue #54070; async `generateMetadata` workarounds stopped working on Next 15.5.6+). We're on 16.2.1, so the rendered tags drop the slash unilaterally — and the prior phase had aligned JSON-LD to `https://innerzero.com/` (with slash), creating drift in the opposite direction. **Fix:** flipped `absoluteUrl("/")` in `src/lib/metadata.ts` to return bare `SITE_URL` (no slash) so JSON-LD now matches what Next emits for canonical/og:url byte-for-byte. Updated all 10 hardcoded `@id` JSON-LD fields across home / about / download / blog/[slug] from `"https://innerzero.com/#organization"` to `` `${absoluteUrl("/")}#organization` `` template literals, so cross-page graph-stitching (publisher/author/sameAs references) stays consistent if SITE_URL ever changes. llms.txt and llms-full.txt root references flipped to the no-slash form. Google treats both root forms as equivalent for canonical purposes — the SEO win is internal consistency, not direction. Doc comment in metadata.ts now explains the flip rationale so a future reader doesn't "fix" it back. No redirects, middleware, sitemap, robots, or `metadataBase` touched. | COMPLETE 2026-04-21 |
| **SEO canonical URL unification** | Fix for Google Search Console "Duplicate, Google chose different canonical than user" on the homepage, caused by trailing-slash drift between the page's own URL signals. Metadata-level audit traced it to: `alternates.canonical: "/"` resolving via `metadataBase` to `https://innerzero.com/` (with slash) while `openGraph.url` and three JSON-LD `url` fields hardcoded `https://innerzero.com` (no slash). Google saw mixed signals and picked its own canonical (the www variant, because the www→non-www 308 redirect existed but Google's last www crawl predated it). Redirects / middleware / sitemap / robots were confirmed clean and left untouched — this is a metadata fix only. **Site-wide convention established:** root URL always `https://innerzero.com/` (trailing slash), sub-pages always `https://innerzero.com/<path>` (no trailing slash), host always apex (never www). Matches what Next.js emits natively under `trailingSlash: false` and what the apex redirect lands on. **`src/lib/metadata.ts` additive extension:** new exported `absoluteUrl(path)` helper that normalises any path to the convention (root → `${SITE_URL}/`, sub-paths → `${SITE_URL}/<trimmed>`); `createMetadata` signature widened to accept optional `path?: string` which, when provided, auto-fills `alternates.canonical` (relative) and `openGraph.url` (absolute via `absoluteUrl`). Explicit caller values always win — precedence is caller > path-derived > undefined — so every existing callsite that already sets these fields keeps identical output. Mental-diffed three-plus existing callers pre/post (about, changelog, download, blog) to confirm backwards-compat. **Homepage migration (`src/app/(marketing)/page.tsx`):** switched to `createMetadata({ path: "/" })`, dropped the broken hardcoded `openGraph.url`, and replaced the three homepage-referring JSON-LD `url` fields (Organization, SoftwareApplication, WebSite) plus `downloadUrl`, `logo.url`, and the WebSite `potentialAction.target.urlTemplate` with `absoluteUrl(...)` calls so every URL the page emits for itself renders as `https://innerzero.com/`. **Drift fixes on four other pages:** `/about` JSON-LD Organization `url` + `logo.url` updated; `/download` JSON-LD SoftwareApplication `url` + `downloadUrl` + `publisher.url` updated; `/features` JSON-LD SoftwareApplication `url` updated; `/blog/[slug]` generateMetadata `url` + JSON-LD BlogPosting `image` + `author.url` + `publisher.url` + `logo.url` + `mainEntityOfPage.@id` all rewritten to use `absoluteUrl`. All routed through the new helper rather than hardcoded strings so any future SITE_URL env override stays consistent. **llms.txt / llms-full.txt:** three bare `https://innerzero.com` references (homepage citations) rewritten to `https://innerzero.com/` to match the convention; all sub-page refs were already correct; zero `www.innerzero.com` references on site (grep confirmed). **Scope guards held:** no redirects added or modified (next.config.ts, vercel.json, middleware all untouched); `trailingSlash` setting unchanged; `next-sitemap.config.js`, `robots.txt` unchanged; other pages' canonical patterns left as-is (no mass migration to the `path` signature — that's a follow-up refactor); `createMetadata` not renamed; `DEFAULT_METADATA` merge behaviour unchanged; `SITE_URL` stays in metadata.ts. **Surprise noted in the audit:** `DEFAULT_METADATA.openGraph.url` IS set today (to bare `SITE_URL`, no slash), which would theoretically leak through if a page omitted `openGraph.url`, but every `createMetadata` caller already sets it explicitly so the fallback is effectively dead code; flagged but out of scope. **Validation:** mental diff of about/changelog/download/blog confirmed byte-identical `createMetadata` output pre/post for the unchanged callers; no `npm run build` run per explicit scope guardrail (static-edits-only); grep of `url: "https://innerzero.com"` returns zero matches in `src/**`; remaining literal `"https://innerzero.com"` references are all `SITE_URL` base constants (or the CORS allow-list in `auth-desktop.ts`) which concatenate sub-paths later and are correct without a trailing slash. | COMPLETE 2026-04-21 |

| **W3C feed validator: HTML-encode inside CDATA** | Follow-up to the earlier CDATA wrap that cleared the bulk of the W3C recommendations but left one "Named entity expected. Got none." warning at `/feed.xml` line 191. Root cause: RSS readers HTML-parse `<description>` content after the XML parser decodes CDATA, so a bare `Q&A` (in the "things you can do with InnerZero" blog excerpt) tripped the second parser even though CDATA made it XML-valid. **Fix — `src/lib/feeds.ts::toRssCdata`:** payload is now HTML-encoded (`&` → `&amp;`, `<` → `&lt;`, `>` → `&gt;`) **before** the CDATA wrap and the `]]>` split guard. `&` pass is conservative — negative lookahead `(?!(?:[a-zA-Z]+|#\d+|#x[0-9a-fA-F]+);)` skips any ampersand that is already leading a valid HTML entity, so upstream-escaped content (e.g. `&amp;`) survives unchanged rather than becoming `&amp;amp;`. Apostrophes and quotes untouched (CDATA covers them at the XML layer; HTML text content doesn't require them encoded). Order `&` → `<` → `>` is mandatory — swapping would re-escape the `&` characters introduced by the angle-bracket passes. After encoding, the `]]>` split check is defensive-only since `>` → `&gt;` already removes the pattern. Empty-input, whitespace-only-trims-to-empty, and `]]>` guards all retained. **Unchanged:** `xmlEscape` (stays responsible for title / link / guid / pubDate / category), `buildExcerpt` ordering (strip → truncate → plain), `stripMarkdown`, `truncateAtWord`, `toRfc822`, `absoluteUrl`, `SITE_URL`. JSON Feed (`/api/feed`) untouched per scope — it has no HTML parse layer. Response headers / revalidate / Content-Type unchanged on both RSS routes. **Scope guards held:** zero npm packages added; `getAllPostsForFeed` signature unchanged; no changes to `feed.xml/route.ts` or `changelog.xml/route.ts` (they already call `toRssCdata` from the prior fix). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean. Local `next start` + curl on `/feed.xml`: 27 CDATA `<description>` blocks (26 items + 1 channel), zero non-CDATA descriptions, `Q&A` now emitted as `Q&amp;A` inside CDATA at line 191 (where the validator flagged it). Zero mid-entity slices in output. Nine-case unit test confirmed: bare `Q&A` → `Q&amp;A`; already-encoded `Q&amp;A` → `Q&amp;A` (no double); `&#8212;` numeric and `&#x2014;` hex entities pass through; mixed `AT&T &amp; Q&A` correctly encodes only the two bare ampersands; `<div>` → `&lt;div&gt;`; empty + whitespace both emit `<![CDATA[]]>`; `before]]>after` ends up as `before]]&gt;after` (the `>` pass resolves the CDATA-collision without needing the split guard). `/changelog.xml` re-checked, no regression (release descriptions have no bare ampersands today, but the helper now protects them if one ever appears). Expected W3C revalidation against `https://innerzero.com/feed.xml` after deploy: valid RSS, zero recommendations. | COMPLETE 2026-04-19 |
| **Blog category filter: replace overflow-x-auto with flex-wrap** | The blog tag filter on `/blog` was using `flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 mb-8 scrollbar-hide` with `shrink-0` pills, so the row of 14 categories overflowed past the viewport on common widths. The `scrollbar-hide` utility removed the scrollbar visual, leaving users with no indication that the list extended further — at 1280px the rightmost pill ("claude") rendered half-cut and "voice" was entirely off-screen. **Fix:** [src/app/(marketing)/blog/BlogGrid.tsx:26](src/app/(marketing)/blog/BlogGrid.tsx#L26) — single class swap from `flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2 mb-8 scrollbar-hide` to `flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-8`. Wrapping flips the failure mode: when total label width exceeds container width, pills now flow onto the next row instead of being clipped. Existing `gap-2` provides both horizontal AND vertical spacing once flex-wrap engages (Tailwind/CSS gap is axis-agnostic on flex). The `shrink-0` on each pill stays — harmless under wrap (pills retain natural size; shrink prevention was already correct for both modes), and removing it would be churn for no benefit. The `pb-2` (was scrollbar clearance) and `scrollbar-hide` (custom utility for the now-gone scroll affordance) both dropped since they're meaningless without the scroll. **Constraint compliance:** zero new design tokens; pill design (rounded-full, px-4 py-1.5, text-xs, accent-gold active state) untouched; category list and ordering untouched (server-driven from `getAllTags()`); no copy or em-dash regressions; Tailwind utilities only. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean — only warning is the same pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace-root inference notice. Playwright DOM probe at every breakpoint (button rect grouped by Y to count rows, `document.documentElement.scrollWidth > window.innerWidth` checked for horizontal scroll): 375px → 5 rows of 4/3/3/3/1 pills, no horizontal scroll, all 14 categories tappable; 768px → 2 balanced rows of 7/7, no scroll; 1280px → 2 rows of 12/2 ("claude" + "voice" cleanly on row 2 — was the cut-off pair pre-fix), no scroll; 1920px → max-w-7xl Container caps the row at 1216px so the full label run still wraps to 2 rows of 13/1 (the natural label widths slightly exceed 1216px once "All" + 13 tags are summed with gap-2 between each — wrap behaviour is correct, "1 row likely" in the spec was a soft expectation not a hard requirement, max-w-7xl cap explicitly preferred over edge-to-edge stretch). Active state on "All" retains its gold highlight; hover and focus states unchanged. The post grid below repaints lower by ~30 px on 1280 / ~70 px on 375 to accommodate the extra rows — acceptable per the spec's "do not constrain height to fit one row" rule. | COMPLETE 2026-04-26 |
| **Homepage Integrations section + theme toggle moved to far-right desktop edge** | Two coupled changes. **(A) Integrations section:** new `<IntegrationsSection>` component placed between `<PrivacyStatement>` and `<HomeFAQ>` on the homepage (matches the user's suggested flow Hero → Features → How it works → Privacy → Integrations → FAQ). One `<h2>` "Works with what you already use" + neutral lead "Plug in your favourite AI models or sync your data. All optional, all under your control." (no em dashes). Two `<h3>` subgroups: "AI models" (8 cards: ChatGPT/OpenAI, Claude/Anthropic, Gemini/Google, DeepSeek, Grok/xAI, Qwen/Alibaba, Kimi/Moonshot, Ollama/Local models) and "Your data, on your terms" (3 cards: Google Calendar/Two-way sync, Gmail/Read-only, Telegram/Bot). Card names are `<h4>` for clean h2→h3→h4 outline. **Discord deliberately omitted:** verified `integrations/connectors/loader.py` in the InnerZero desktop repo — Discord is commented-out scaffolding for a future Phase 98.8 (`# Phase 98.8 adds: "integrations.connectors.discord"`), no `discord.py` exists. Per task rule "If only scaffolding, omit". **Logos sourced from svgl.app (MIT licensed):** `openai.svg` + `openai_dark.svg` (no openai_white variant exists, dark variant is the white version), `anthropic_white.svg` + `anthropic_black.svg`, `gemini.svg` (full colour, single file), `deepseek.svg` (#4D6BFE blue, single), `xai_light.svg` + `xai_dark.svg`, `qwen_light.svg` + `qwen_dark.svg`, `kimi-icon.svg` (self-contained black rounded square works on both themes), `ollama_light.svg` + `ollama_dark.svg`, `google-calendar.svg` (full colour), `gmail.svg` (Google brand colours), `telegram.svg` (blue gradient). All bundled in `/public/images/integrations/` per WEBCLAUDE.md "no external CDNs" rule, served as static assets via `next/image` with `unoptimized` (SVGs don't need raster optimisation). Total payload ≈ 35 KB across 15 SVG files (mono brands keep both light/dark variants). **Theme-aware logo swap:** for the 5 mono-only brands (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, Qwen, Ollama) two `<Image>` tags render with class `theme-dark-glyph` and `theme-light-glyph`. Two CSS rules added to `globals.css` (just before "Skip to content" block, outside the `prefers-reduced-motion` block since they're not animations): `.theme-dark-glyph { display: block }` / `.theme-light-glyph { display: none }` flipped under `[data-theme="light"]`. Consistent with the project's existing pattern of small custom utility classes (`mobile-nav-enter`, `theme-icon-rotate`, `faq-content`). Pair lives outside `@theme inline` — these are display utilities, not design tokens. Verified live in Playwright: in dark theme 0 light-glyph imgs visible / 5 dark-glyph imgs visible; in light theme exact opposite. Single-variant brands (Gemini, DeepSeek, Kimi, Calendar, Gmail, Telegram) render the same SVG on both themes — full-colour brand marks remain legible against either bg-card. **Card design:** `rounded-xl border border-border-default bg-bg-card p-6` with `hover:border-border-hover hover:bg-bg-card-hover` matching the existing card-hover pattern from FeatureCards. Logo centred at `h-12 w-12`, name `<h4>` `mt-4 text-base font-semibold text-text-primary`, sublabel `<p>` `mt-1 text-sm text-text-muted`. Cards are non-interactive `<div>`s per spec (no link/button wrapping). Each logo wrapper carries `role="img" aria-label="ChatGPT logo"` etc. with both child `<Image>`s using `alt=""` + `aria-hidden="true"` to avoid double-announcing in screen readers. **Grid:** `grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 sm:gap-4 md:grid-cols-4`. Verified in Playwright: 375px → 2 cols, 12px gap, no horizontal scroll (docWidth 360 < viewport 375); 768px → 4 cols (md is the same as desktop); 1280px → 4 cols, 16px gap; 1920px → grid width 1216px inside max-w-7xl Container, centred not stretched. Section spacing `py-12 md:py-20` matches the existing rhythm. `<ScrollReveal>` wrapping the heading and each subgroup matches every other homepage section's reveal cadence. **(B) Theme toggle desktop reposition:** Header.tsx desktop right cluster reordered from `ThemeToggle | (Discord+GitHub) | HeaderAuth | Download` to `(Discord+GitHub) | HeaderAuth | Download | ThemeToggle`. ThemeToggle wrapped in `<div className="-ml-1">` (canonical form of `ml-[-4px]`) so the effective gap to Download is 12px (gap-3) instead of the parent's gap-4 (16px) — the "edge utility" tightening the user specified. Tab order in DOM exactly matches visual left-to-right: `Logo → 5 nav links → Discord → GitHub → Log In → Download Free → Switch theme` — verified live by walking `header.querySelectorAll('a, button')` and inspecting the rendered order. Mobile bar untouched (Logo + compact Download + hamburger), drawer untouched (ThemeToggle still inside, separated from socials by `ml-auto` per the original spec). **Constraint compliance:** zero new design tokens (the two `.theme-*-glyph` classes are display utilities matching the existing custom-class convention, not colour/spacing/font tokens); Tailwind utility classes only on the new component; no em dashes in any new copy on the page (`grep "—" src/components/sections/IntegrationsSection.tsx` → zero); existing button spec / Container / ScrollReveal / Card hover tokens reused; no new dependencies (lucide-react and next/image already present); no `"use client"` added (IntegrationsSection is a server component since it has no interactivity, only the dual `<Image>`s with theme classes which CSS handles entirely client-side without React state); `next.config.ts` `images` block untouched since `/public/` paths don't need `remotePatterns`. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean — only warning is the same pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace-root inference notice. Console clean on `/` page load. Visual verification complete in dark + light themes at 375 / 768 / 1280 / 1920 px (screenshots taken; all 11 logos render correctly on both bg-card variants in both themes; theme-aware swap confirmed via DOM probe). **WEBCLAUDE.md updates:** brand-identity table gains an "Integrations" row; file-structure tree gains `/public/images/integrations/` annotation. | COMPLETE 2026-04-26 |
| **Header restructure: persistent Download Free CTA, theme toggle separated, lg breakpoint, accessible drawer** | Right cluster previously read as "Discord, GitHub, ThemeToggle, [LogIn+DownloadFree if logged out / Account if logged in], Hamburger" with everything visible from the smallest viewport upward, the theme toggle nestled between the socials and the auth area, and the Download Free button only existing for signed-out users. Three goals: (a) Download Free always present, (b) theme toggle reads as a utility control rather than a third social, (c) clean mobile bar. **Breakpoint flip md→lg:** old code switched between mobile and desktop layouts at 768px which crowded the bar (5 nav items + 5 right-cluster items + logo wouldn't fit cleanly until ~1024px). New layout switches at lg (1024px), so 768-1023 viewports get the mobile bar (logo + compact Download + hamburger) and ≥1024 gets the full desktop strip. Verified at 768/1280: nothing crowds. **Desktop right cluster (`hidden lg:flex items-center gap-4`):** ThemeToggle → tight social pair (`gap-2`) wrapping Discord + GitHub → HeaderAuth → Download Free `<Button>`. Asymmetric gaps (`gap-4` between groups, `gap-2` inside the social pair) signal grouping by spacing alone — no border/divider element introduced because the spacing rhythm already does the job and stays consistent with the existing token system. Tab order in DOM matches the spec (logo → nav links → theme → socials → account → download). **`HeaderAuth.tsx` refactor:** dropped the embedded Download Free button (it now lives in Header.tsx so it's visible logged-in too); component now returns just the Account link (logged in) or Log In link (logged out); removed the `hidden md:block`/`hidden md:flex` visibility wrappers so HeaderAuth is visibility-agnostic and can be reused inside the mobile drawer if needed. Loading placeholder shrunk from `w-[130px]` → `w-22` (88px, matches Account link width to avoid layout shift). **Mobile bar (`flex lg:hidden items-center gap-2`):** Logo (left) → compact `<Button className="text-sm px-3 py-1.5">Download</Button>` (gold, "Download" not "Download Free" to fit the tighter space) → hamburger trigger. At 375px the three items measure logo 16-147 / Download 209-300 / hamburger 308-344, all visible with the right edge clearing the viewport at x=344 inside a 375px window — no truncation. Header stays at 64px on every breakpoint. **`MobileNav.tsx` extension:** trigger now declares `aria-expanded={open}` and `aria-controls={drawerId}` (drawerId via `useId`) and the drawer carries `role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-hidden={!open}`. Drawer body restructured per spec: nav links (existing block tap-targets, `py-3`) → border-t divider → Account or Log In link (with User icon, same py-3 block target as nav links so logged-in users have a tap-sized way in) → border-t divider → bottom row containing the social pair (Discord + GitHub at 40×40 tap targets with `gap-2`) and the ThemeToggle pushed right via `ml-auto` so it visibly separates from the socials matching the desktop's "utility ≠ social" grouping principle. The previous bottom Download Free button was removed from inside the drawer because Download is now in the bar. Auth state pulled with the same Supabase client logic as HeaderAuth (intentional duplication of ~10 lines rather than introducing a useUser hook for a one-off case). **Keyboard behaviour:** when the drawer opens a `useEffect` queries the first focusable inside (`a, button, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])`) and calls `.focus()` on it (lands on the X button); a separate `useEffect` registers a `keydown` listener for the open phase that handles Escape (closes drawer + restores focus to the trigger via `triggerRef.current?.focus()`) and Tab cycling (Shift+Tab on first focusable → last, Tab on last focusable → first). All verified live in Playwright at 375px viewport: drawer opens via click, `aria-expanded` flips true, focus lands on Close, Shift+Tab from Close cycles to ThemeToggle (last focusable), Tab from ThemeToggle cycles back to Close, Escape closes the drawer + flips `aria-expanded` to false + restores focus to the trigger button, X button click also closes cleanly. **Constraint compliance:** zero new design tokens (asymmetric gap-only signals grouping; the divider was the user's first-preference option but I went with their explicit fallback `gap-4` outer / `gap-2` inner since it's lighter visually and preserves the existing token surface); Tailwind utility classes only; existing button spec reused (`<Button href="/download">` for desktop, same with `text-sm px-3 py-1.5` override for compact mobile via twMerge); icon library remains lucide-react + the existing custom DiscordIcon/GitHubIcon SVGs (no new deps); no `"use client"` added to anything that didn't already have it (Header, MobileNav, ThemeToggle, HeaderAuth were all already client because they hold state); section padding (`px-4 sm:px-6 lg:px-8`) and 64px header height preserved. **Validation:** `npm run build` clean — only warning is the same pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace-root inference notice. Playwright DOM probe at 1280: header height 64, items at x=32 (Logo) / 348-639 (nav links) / 863 (Theme) / 915 (Discord) / 959 (GitHub) / 1011 (Log In) / 1093 (Download Free, w=140). Gap measurements: theme→discord 16px, discord→github 8px, github→login 16px, login→download 17px — exactly the gap-4/gap-2/gap-4/gap-4 cascade the spec called for. At 1920 the inner container caps at max-w-7xl (1280px) and is centred. At 768 only logo + Download + hamburger render in the bar (drawer items measured `right` value off-screen, `transform: translate*` from `mobile-nav-enter` confirms closed state). At 375 all three bar items fit, no truncation. | COMPLETE 2026-04-26 |
| **Pricing hero h1 wrap fix: text-balance + block span** | Awkward wrap on `/pricing` h1: "Free to download. Optional extras when you want them." was breaking after "extras" leaving "when you want them." stranded on a short second line. **Primary fix:** parent wrapper `mx-auto max-w-3xl text-center px-4` → `text-center px-4` (drop the parent max-width); h1 gains `mx-auto max-w-4xl text-balance`; subtitle `<p>` gains explicit `mx-auto max-w-2xl` so subtitle width stays narrower than h1 (preserves prior subtitle wrap rhythm now that parent constraint is gone). **Fallback escalation:** `text-balance` alone produced an awkward "Free to download. Optional / extras when you want them." split at 1280px and 768px because text-wrap:balance optimises for equal-length lines, not sentence boundaries (verified via Playwright DOM probe at 1280, 768, 375 — every test produced the bad split). Applied user's specified `block sm:inline` span on sentence 2 — verified at 1280px the desktop wrap was still awkward because at sm+ the span is inline and balance still controlled the wrap. Escalated to unconditional `<span className="block text-balance">Optional extras when you want them.</span>` which forces the break at every viewport and lets balance handle within-sentence wrapping when sentence 2 itself wraps. **Verification (Playwright DOM Range probe per viewport, font / h1 width / line text dumped):** 375px → 3 lines, sentence 1 clean, sentence 2 wraps to "Optional extras when" / "you want them." (4/3 word balance) — within user's "may wrap to 2 lines" allowance; 1280px → 2 clean lines, one per sentence; 1920px → 2 clean lines, h1 capped at 896px (max-w-4xl), not edge-to-edge. **768px caveat:** Playwright headless renders an inset vertical scrollbar at this viewport which trims the h1 container to 721px; sentence 2's natural width at 40px Inter Bold is 726px (probed by cloning the span with white-space:nowrap and measuring). Off by 5-6px → wraps to 3 lines in this specific render. At 800px the same h1 measures 753px and sentence 2 fits on one line. Real iPad and Android tablets at 768px use overlay scrollbars (no width subtraction) so the h1 container is 736px which fits sentence 2's 726px. Logged this scrollbar-edge as expected behaviour rather than escalating to `lg:` font breakpoint or `px-3` padding (either would technically meet the verification spec but would change the visual rhythm at all tablet sizes for one borderline viewport). **Constraints held:** no copy changes (headline still "Free to download. Optional extras when you want them."); no new font sizes (text-3xl mobile / md:text-[2.5rem] desktop unchanged); Tailwind utilities only, no inline styles or new CSS; centring preserved (mx-auto on h1 and p instead of parent); section padding preserved (px-4 stays). **Validation:** `npm run build` clean — only warning is the same pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace-root inference notice from prior commits, unrelated. | COMPLETE 2026-04-26 |
| **"free forever" sweep across non-pricing surfaces** | Follow-up to commit 4eabc98 which de-permanenced the pricing page only. Branding goal: stop committing to permanence ("forever"/"for life"/"always free") site-wide while keeping the no-cost-no-catch promise intact. **Inventory established by `grep -rni "free forever" src/ content/ public/`:** 13 hits across 9 source files plus 8 hits across the two AI-discovery feeds in `public/`. `src/components/sections/Hero.tsx` itself has no occurrence; the homepage hero copy is driven by `HERO.description` in `src/lib/constants.ts:41`, so editing the constant covers the homepage. `/for/researchers` and `/for/offline-work` had no matches and were not touched. Two surfaces beyond the user-listed expected set were caught and edited: `src/app/for/PersonaPage.tsx` (the shared persona-page component, used as a template), and the two `public/llms*.txt` feeds (LLM-discovery copies of pricing claims). **`src/lib/constants.ts` HERO.description:** `". Free forever. No cloud required."` flipped to `". Free to download. No cloud required."` matching the pricing-page hero convention. Drives the homepage hero. **`src/components/sections/CTABanner.tsx`:** default `description` prop flipped from `"InnerZero is free forever. Download and start chatting in minutes."` to `"InnerZero is free to download. Start chatting in minutes."` — used on `/about` and the homepage CTA banner. **`src/app/for/PersonaPage.tsx:284`:** "Ready to try it?" footer copy flipped from `"Free forever for personal use. No account required."` to `"Free for personal use. No account required."` — applies to every `/for/*` persona page that doesn't override the footer. **`/for/students/page.tsx`:** `leadFit` (line 29) flipped from `"InnerZero is free forever, runs fully on your laptop..."` to `"InnerZero is free, runs fully on your laptop..."`; `whyBullets[0].title` (line 32) flipped from `"Free forever"` to `"Free to download"` so the bullet still reads as a price-style label. **`/for/writers/page.tsx:52`:** `whyBullets` title flipped from `"Free forever, no deadline paywall"` to `"Free, no deadline paywall"`. **`/for/developers/page.tsx:39`:** detail copy flipped from `"...Free forever, no per-seat billing."` to `"...Free, no per-seat billing."` **`/what-is-local-ai/page.tsx`:** three hits — persona one-liner (line 74) `"Free forever, no account..."` → `"Free, no account..."`; comparison-table local-cost cell (line 152) `"Free forever. Electricity is the only marginal cost."` → `"Free. Electricity is the only marginal cost."`; FAQ answer (line 442) reflowed to drop `"InnerZero itself is free forever for personal use."` → `"InnerZero itself is free for personal use."` (line wrap reflowed to 78 cols to match surrounding paragraph style). **`/models/page.tsx:172`:** intro paragraph reflowed: `"Local AI is free forever, no subscription, no account required."` → `"Local AI is free, no subscription, no account required."`. **Blog MDX posts:** both `src/content/blog/why-we-built-innerzero.mdx:47` and `src/content/blog/innerzero-is-live.mdx:50` are brand-positioning claims, not quotes of the literal phrase, so they fall under the marketing-copy rule rather than the historical-quotation exception. why-we-built: `"The local app will stay free forever."` → `"The local app stays free."` (next sentence "The core local experience never gets paywalled" already carries the long-term promise without permanence-of-pricing wording). innerzero-is-live: `"The local app is free forever. No account, no subscription, no trial."` matching the new PRICING_FREE.subtitle. **`WEBCLAUDE.md` brand-identity table (line 57):** intentionally left as `"Free forever — no subscription, no trial, no account required"` because the user's grep scope was `src/ content/ public/` and explicitly excluded the project-guide doc; flagged for a separate decision. **Constraint compliance:** zero em dashes in any new or replacement copy (verified by grep on the diff); no Tailwind tokens or class changes; pricing page and PRICING_FREE constant untouched (sole reverification: `git diff` shows zero changes under `src/components/sections/PricingSection.tsx`, `src/app/(marketing)/pricing/`, or PRICING_FREE block in constants.ts); existing layout, ordering, and component boundaries preserved everywhere; line wraps in `/what-is-local-ai/page.tsx:441-444` and `/models/page.tsx:171-173` reflowed only to keep the file's existing 78-col wrap discipline after the shorter substitution. **Validation:** `grep -rni "free forever\|always free\|free for life\|never paid" src/ public/` returns zero matches; `npm run build` clean — only warning is the same pre-existing `turbopack.root` workspace-root inference notice from 4eabc98, unrelated.
